### Phase 97: 3D Spherical Spiral / Loxodrome Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedInSphericalSpiral` / `Ctrl+Alt+W`)
- [x] **97.1. Pole-to-Pole Spherical Spiral Distribution (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInSphericalSpiral`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInSphericalSpiral(Radius, Turns, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms along a continuous 3D pole-to-pole spherical spiral on a sphere of radius $R$ centered at the active selection pivot ($u_i = 2t_i - 1 \in [-1, 1], \; \rho_i = \sqrt{1 - u_i^2}, \; \theta_i = 2\pi \cdot \text{Turns} \cdot t_i$) with outward spherical normal alignment (`OrientMode = 1`) or analytical 3D spherical spiral tangent alignment (`OrientMode = 2`).
  - Added **`Sphere Spiral...`** (`ButtonArrangeSphericalSpiral` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges along a 2-turn spherical spiral with outward normal alignment; `Ctrl`-click orients along the 3D spiral tangent; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeInSphericalSpiralDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+W`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 97):** [CGE 3D Transforms (`TCastleTransform`)](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Blender 4 Array Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/generate/array.html) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Godot 4 Using 3D Transforms](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/3d/using_transforms.html)

### Phase 98: 3D Superellipse / Squircle / Astroid Star Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedInSuperellipse` / `Ctrl+Alt+E`)
- [x] **98.1. Lamé Superellipse, Squircle Stadium & 4-Cusped Astroid Star Distribution (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInSuperellipse`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInSuperellipse(RadiusX, RadiusZ, ExponentN, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms around a 3D Lamé curve / Superellipse on the horizontal XZ plane centered at the active selection pivot ($x_i = C_x + R_x\operatorname{sgn}(\cos\theta_i)|\cos\theta_i|^{2/n}, \; z_i = C_z + R_z\operatorname{sgn}(\sin\theta_i)|\sin\theta_i|^{2/n}$), covering 4-cusped Astroid stars ($n = 2/3$), diamonds ($n = 1$), ellipses ($n = 2$), and rounded squircle stadiums ($n = 4$), with `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = inward XZ, `2` = outward XZ).

### Phase 100: 3D Circular Arc Bend / Curvature Deformation (`BendSelected` / `Ctrl+Alt+A`)
- [x] **100.1. Circular Arc Bend Deformation Along Spine Axis (`TCastleTransformManipulate.BendSelected`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.BendSelected(SpineAxis, BendDirAxis, TotalAngleRad, RotateOrientations): Boolean`: bends 2+ top-level selected transforms distributed along `SpineAxis` into a circular arc of total angle `TotalAngleRad` curving toward `BendDirAxis` around the active selection pivot ($R = L_{\text{spine}} / \text{TotalAngleRad}, \; s' = R\sin\theta_i, \; b' = R(1 - \cos\theta_i) + \Delta b_i\cos\theta_i$) while preserving the third perpendicular axis and optionally rotating each transform's 3D orientation by $\theta_i$ around $\mathbf{e}_{\text{spine}} \times \mathbf{e}_{\text{bend}}$.
  - Added **`Bend Arc...`** (`ButtonBendSelection` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click bends $90^\circ$ along Y toward X; `Ctrl`-click bends $180^\circ$ along X toward Y; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowBendSelectionDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+A`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.

### Phase 101: 3D Viviani Curve / Spherical Figure-8 Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedInVivianiCurve` / `Ctrl+Alt+Q`)

### Phase 104: 3D Spherical Spherify / Bulge / Pinch Deformation (`SpherifySelected` / `Ctrl+Alt+X`)
- [x] **104.1. Spherical Bulge / Pinch / Spherify Deformation (`TCastleTransformManipulate.SpherifySelected`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.SpherifySelected(TargetRadius, StrengthFactor, OrientRadially): Boolean`: deforms 2+ top-level selected transforms by blending their world positions toward (`StrengthFactor > 0`, with `1.0` = 100% spherical projection and `0.5` = 50% spherical bulge) or away from (`StrengthFactor < 0`, pinch/implode) a bounding sphere of radius `TargetRadius` (`0` = auto mean radial distance) centered at the active selection pivot ($\mathbf{P}'_i = \operatorname{Lerp}(\text{StrengthFactor}, \mathbf{P}_i, \mathbf{C} + R\hat{\mathbf{d}}_i)$), optionally orienting each transform's world $+Y$ Up radially outward (`OrientRadially = true`).
  - Added **`Spherify / Bulge...`** (`ButtonSpherifySelection` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click applies 100% spherify + radial outward orientation; `Ctrl`-click applies a 50% spherical bulge; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowSpherifySelectionDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+X`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 104):** [CGE Transform Manipulation](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Blender 4 Simple Deform Modifier (Twist/Bend/Taper/Stretch)](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/simple_deform.html) · [Blender 4 Cast Modifier (Sphere/Cylinder/Cuboid)](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/cast.html) · [Blender 4 Wave Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/wave.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [Blender Simple Deform Overview](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_intro.png) · [Blender Twist](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_twist.png) · [Blender Bend](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_bend.png) · [Blender Cast](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_cast_example.jpg) · [Blender Circular Wave](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_wave_example-circular.jpg)
  - **Example (Phase 104):** Select 2+ `TCastleTransform` nodes in the 3D Viewport and press **`Ctrl+Alt+X`** or call `SpherifySelected` (`Click` for default deformation, `Ctrl`-click for secondary preset/normal tilt, `Shift`-click for the interactive parameter dialog).

### Phase 105: 3D Möbius Strip / Twisted Ribbon Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedInMobiusStrip` / `Ctrl+Alt+Y`)
- [x] **105.1. Parametric Möbius Strip & Twisted Ribbon Distribution (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInMobiusStrip`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInMobiusStrip(MajorRadius, StripHalfWidth, HalfTwists, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms along a 3D Möbius Strip / Twisted Ribbon around the active selection pivot ($x_i = C_x + (R + v_i\cos\alpha_i)\cos u_i, \; y_i = C_y + v_i\sin\alpha_i, \; z_i = C_z + (R + v_i\cos\alpha_i)\sin u_i$ with $\alpha_i = \text{HalfTwists} \cdot u_i / 2$ and alternating transverse offset $v_i \in [-W, +W]$), with `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = twisting ribbon normal $\mathbf{N}_i$ as world $+Y$ Up and centerline tangent $\mathbf{T}_i$ as forward, `2` = inward toward center).
  - Added **`Mobius Strip...`** (`ButtonArrangeMobius` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges with 1 half-twist and twisting surface normal; `Ctrl`-click arranges with 3 half-twists; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeInMobiusStripDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Y`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 105):** [CGE Visual Editor Manual](https://castle-engine.io/manual_editor.php) · [CGE Viewport & Scenes](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Blender 4 3D Snapping & Gizmos](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/controls/snapping.html) · [Unity Positioning GameObjects](https://docs.unity3d.com/Manual/PositioningGameObjects.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE 3D Editor](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/fps_editor.webp) · [Blender 4 Snapping Menu](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/editors_3dview_controls_snapping_element-menu.png) · [Unity Handle Position Toggles](https://docs.unity3d.com/uploads/Main/gizmo-handle-position-toggles.png)
  - **Example (Phase 105):** Select target `TCastleTransform` component(s) in `castle-editor` and press **`Ctrl+Alt+Y`** or call `ArrangeSelectedInMobiusStrip` from `PanelLayoutTransform` to apply the transformation as a single Undo-able step.

### Phase 106: 3D Parametric Heart & Cardioid Curve Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedInCardioidOrHeart` / `Ctrl+Alt+Z`)
- [x] **106.1. Algebraic Heart Curve & Classical Cardioid Distribution (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInCardioidOrHeart`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInCardioidOrHeart(SizeScale, HeartShape, VerticalWave, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms along either a 3D Algebraic Heart Curve ($x_i = C_x + S\sin^3 t_i, \; z_i = C_z + \frac{S}{16}(13\cos t_i - 5\cos 2t_i - 2\cos 3t_i - \cos 4t_i)$ when `HeartShape = true`) or a Classical Cardioid ($r_i = \frac{S}{2}(1 - \sin t_i)$ when `HeartShape = false`), with optional vertical wave $y_i = C_y + \text{VerticalWave}\cdot\sin(2t_i)$ and `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = inward XZ, `2` = outward XZ).
  - Added **`Heart / Cardioid...`** (`ButtonArrangeHeart` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges in a Heart curve facing inward; `Ctrl`-click arranges in a Cardioid curve facing inward; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeInCardioidOrHeartDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Z`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - Added automated unit test `TestSpherifyMobiusStripAndCardioidHeart` in `tests/code/testcases/testcastletransformmanipulate.pas`.

### Phase 108: 3D Radial Ripple / Concentric Wave Deformation (`RippleSelected` / `Ctrl+Alt+;`)
- [x] **108.1. Damped Radial Sinusoidal Wave Deformation (`TCastleTransformManipulate.RippleSelected`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.RippleSelected(Amplitude, Wavelength, PhaseRad, DecayRate, TiltNormals): Boolean`: deforms 2+ top-level selected transforms by applying a damped concentric sinusoidal wave displacement along the vertical Y axis as a function of radial XZ distance $r_i = \sqrt{(X_i - C_x)^2 + (Z_i - C_z)^2}$ from the active selection pivot ($\Delta Y_i = A \cdot e^{-\alpha r_i} \cdot \cos(k r_i + \phi)$ where $k = 2\pi / \lambda$), optionally tilting each transform's world $+Y$ Up vector to the analytical wave surface normal $\mathbf{N}_i = \operatorname{Normalize}(-\frac{dy}{dr}\frac{\Delta X_i}{r_i},\; 1,\; -\frac{dy}{dr}\frac{\Delta Z_i}{r_i})$.
  - Added **`Ripple Wave...`** (`ButtonRippleSelection` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click applies amplitude $2.0$, wavelength $10.0$ + surface normal tilt; `Ctrl`-click applies damped ripple with decay $0.08$; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowRippleSelectionDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+;`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 108):** [CGE Transform Manipulation](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Blender 4 Simple Deform Modifier (Twist/Bend/Taper/Stretch)](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/simple_deform.html) · [Blender 4 Cast Modifier (Sphere/Cylinder/Cuboid)](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/cast.html) · [Blender 4 Wave Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/wave.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [Blender Simple Deform Overview](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_intro.png) · [Blender Twist](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_twist.png) · [Blender Bend](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_bend.png) · [Blender Cast](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_cast_example.jpg) · [Blender Circular Wave](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_wave_example-circular.jpg)
  - **Example (Phase 108):** Select 2+ `TCastleTransform` nodes in the 3D Viewport and press **`Ctrl+Alt+;`** or call `RippleSelected` (`Click` for default deformation, `Ctrl`-click for secondary preset/normal tilt, `Shift`-click for the interactive parameter dialog).

### Phase 109: 3D Lemniscate of Bernoulli / Infinity Curve ($\infty$) Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedInLemniscate` / `Ctrl+Alt+'`)
- [x] **109.1. Parametric Lemniscate of Bernoulli & Figure-8 Overpass Distribution (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInLemniscate`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInLemniscate(HalfWidthA, VerticalBridgeLift, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms along a 3D Lemniscate of Bernoulli ($\infty$ curve: $x(t) = C_x + \frac{a\cos t}{1 + \sin^2 t}, \; z(t) = C_z + \frac{a\sin t\cos t}{1 + \sin^2 t}, \; y(t) = C_y + H\sin t$) with optional vertical overpass bridge lift $H$ at the central crossing so the two figure-8 tracks pass cleanly over/under one another, and `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = analytical 3D curve tangent, `2` = inward toward center).
  - Added **`Infinity Loop...`** (`ButtonArrangeLemniscate` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges in a flat infinity curve oriented along the 3D curve tangent; `Ctrl`-click adds a $+2.0$ vertical bridge overpass at the center crossing; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeInLemniscateDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+'`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 109):** [CGE 3D Transforms (`TCastleTransform`)](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Blender 4 Array Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/generate/array.html) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Godot 4 Using 3D Transforms](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/3d/using_transforms.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE 3D Transform Scene](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/transform_scene.webp) · [Godot 4 3D Viewport](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/_images/tuto_3d3.webp) · [Blender 4 3D Gizmo](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/editors_3dview_display_gizmo_options-translate.png)
  - **Example (Phase 109):** Select 2+ `TCastleTransform` instances and press **`Ctrl+Alt+'`** or invoke `ArrangeSelectedInLemniscate` from `PanelLayoutTransform` (`Click` for instant parametric placement, `Ctrl`-click to align local `+Y`/`+Z` with surface normals or curve tangents, `Shift`-click for the modal parameter dialog).

### Phase 112: 3D Volume-Preserving Squash & Stretch Deformation (`VolumeSquashStretchSelected` / `Ctrl+Alt+,`)
- [x] **112.1. Volume-Preserving Axial Squash & Stretch (`TCastleTransformManipulate.VolumeSquashStretchSelected`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.VolumeSquashStretchSelected(Axis, StretchFactor, AnchorAtBase, ScaleObjects): Boolean`: applies a volume-preserving 3D squash & stretch deformation along `Axis` (`0=X, 1=Y, 2=Z`) by `StretchFactor` ($s_\parallel = \text{StretchFactor}, \; s_\perp = 1 / \sqrt{\text{StretchFactor}}$ so $s_\parallel \cdot s_\perp^2 = 1$), anchoring longitudinal positions at the minimum coordinate along `Axis` when `AnchorAtBase = true` (so grounded props remain planted at their base) or at the active selection pivot otherwise, and optionally multiplying each transform's local `Scale` by $(s_\parallel, s_\perp, s_\perp)$ when `ScaleObjects = true`.

Fay's Parametric Butterfly Curve around the active selection pivot ($r(t) = e^{\sin t} - 2\cos(4t) + \sin^5\!\left(\frac{2t - \pi}{24}\right), \; x(t) = C_x + S r(t)\cos t, \; z(t) = C_z + S r(t)\sin t, \; y(t) = C_y + \text{WingFlapHeight}\cdot|\cos t|$) with `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = inward XZ, `2` = outward XZ).

### Phase 114: 3D Clélie Curve / Spherical Rosette Orbit Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedInClelieCurve` / `Ctrl+Alt+/`)
- [x] **114.1. Parametric Clélie Spherical Rosette & Polar Satellite Orbit (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInClelieCurve`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInClelieCurve(Radius, PetalRatioM, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms along a 3D Clélie Curve on a sphere of radius $R$ where latitude $\phi = m\theta$ ($x(\theta) = C_x + R\cos(m\theta)\cos\theta, \; y(\theta) = C_y + R\sin(m\theta), \; z(\theta) = C_z + R\cos(m\theta)\sin\theta$), with `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = outward sphere surface normal with analytical Clélie tangent as Up, `2` = analytical 3D Clélie curve tangent with outward sphere normal as Up).
  - Added **`Clelie Sphere...`** (`ButtonArrangeClelie` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges with $m = 2.0$ and outward sphere normal; `Ctrl`-click arranges with $m = 3.0$ and 3D curve tangent; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeInClelieCurveDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+/`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - Added automated unit test `TestSquashStretchButterflyAndClelie` in `tests/code/testcases/testcastletransformmanipulate.pas`.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 114):** [CGE Visual Editor Manual](https://castle-engine.io/manual_editor.php) · [CGE Viewport & Scenes](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Blender 4 3D Snapping & Gizmos](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/controls/snapping.html) · [Unity Positioning GameObjects](https://docs.unity3d.com/Manual/PositioningGameObjects.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE 3D Editor](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/fps_editor.webp) · [Blender 4 Snapping Menu](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/editors_3dview_controls_snapping_element-menu.png) · [Unity Handle Position Toggles](https://docs.unity3d.com/uploads/Main/gizmo-handle-position-toggles.png)
  - **Example (Phase 114):** Select target `TCastleTransform` component(s) in `castle-editor` and press **`Ctrl+Alt+/`** or call `ArrangeSelectedInClelieCurve` from `PanelLayoutTransform` to apply the transformation as a single Undo-able step.

### Phase 116: 3D Radial Vortex / Swirl Deformation (`SwirlSelected` / `Ctrl+Alt+~`)
- [x] **116.1. Distance-Falloff Radial Vortex / Whirlpool Deformation (`TCastleTransformManipulate.SwirlSelected`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.SwirlSelected(Axis, CenterAngleRad, OuterRadius, RotateOrientations): Boolean`: rotates 2+ top-level selected transforms around the line passing through the active selection pivot along `Axis` (`0` = X, `1` = Y, `2` = Z) by a distance-falloff swirl angle $\theta(r_i) = \text{CenterAngleRad}\cdot(1 - \text{Clamp}(r_i / R_{\text{outer}}, 0, 1))$ where $r_i$ is the perpendicular radial distance from the swirl axis (`OuterRadius <= 0` auto-computes from the maximum radial distance of the selection), optionally rotating each transform's 3D world orientation (`RotateOrientations = true`).
  - Added **`Swirl Vortex...`** (`ButtonSwirlSelection` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click swirls $+180^\circ$ around Y; `Ctrl`-click swirls $+360^\circ$ full whirlpool turn; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowSwirlSelectionDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+~`** (`Ctrl+Alt+BackQuote`) in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 116):** [CGE Transform Manipulation](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Blender 4 Simple Deform Modifier (Twist/Bend/Taper/Stretch)](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/simple_deform.html) · [Blender 4 Cast Modifier (Sphere/Cylinder/Cuboid)](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/cast.html) · [Blender 4 Wave Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/wave.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [Blender Simple Deform Overview](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_intro.png) · [Blender Twist](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_twist.png) · [Blender Bend](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_bend.png) · [Blender Cast](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_cast_example.jpg) · [Blender Circular Wave](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_wave_example-circular.jpg)
  - **Example (Phase 116):** Select 2+ `TCastleTransform` nodes in the 3D Viewport and press **`Ctrl+Alt+~`** or call `SwirlSelected` (`Click` for default deformation, `Ctrl`-click for secondary preset/normal tilt, `Shift`-click for the interactive parameter dialog).

### Phase 117: 3D Parametric Trefoil ($3_1$) / Figure-Eight ($4_1$) Knot Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedInKnotCurve` / `Ctrl+Alt+-`)
- [x] **117.1. Classical Trefoil Knot ($3_1$) & Listing's Figure-Eight Knot ($4_1$) (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInKnotCurve`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInKnotCurve(ScaleRadius, KnotType, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms along a 3D Trefoil Knot ($3_1$, `KnotType = 0`: $x = \sin t + 2\sin 2t, \; y = -\sin 3t, \; z = \cos t - 2\cos 2t$) or Figure-Eight Knot ($4_1$, `KnotType = 1`: $x = (2 + \cos 2t)\cos 3t, \; y = \sin 4t, \; z = (2 + \cos 2t)\sin 3t$) scaled by `ScaleRadius`, with `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = analytical 3D knot tangent, `2` = outward radial from knot center).
  - Added **`3D Knot...`** (`ButtonArrangeKnot` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges in a Trefoil knot $3_1$ with 3D tangent orientation; `Ctrl`-click arranges in a Figure-Eight knot $4_1$; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeInKnotCurveDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+-`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 117):** [CGE 3D Transforms (`TCastleTransform`)](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Blender 4 Array Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/generate/array.html) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Godot 4 Using 3D Transforms](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/3d/using_transforms.html)

### Phase 120: 3D Radial Implode / Explode / Gravity Well Falloff Deformation (`RadialFalloffSelected` / `Ctrl+Alt+2`)
- [x] **120.1. Gaussian Distance-Falloff Radial Implode / Explode Deformation (`TCastleTransformManipulate.RadialFalloffSelected`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.RadialFalloffSelected(Strength, FalloffRadius, ScaleWithDisplacement): Boolean`: radially displaces 2+ top-level selected transforms away from (`Strength > 0`, explode / shockwave) or toward (`Strength < 0`, implode / gravity well) the active selection pivot $\mathbf{C}$ with Gaussian distance falloff $d'_i = \max\left(0, d_i + \text{Strength}\cdot\exp(-(d_i / R_{\text{falloff}})^2)\right)$ (`FalloffRadius <= 0` auto-computes from the average distance to $\mathbf{C}$), and optionally modulates each transform's uniform `Scale` proportionally to local falloff intensity (`ScaleWithDisplacement = true`).
  - Added **`Radial Falloff...`** (`ButtonRadialFalloff` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click explodes outward $+3.0$; `Ctrl`-click implodes inward $-3.0$; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowRadialFalloffSelectionDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+2`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 120):** [CGE Transform Manipulation](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Blender 4 Simple Deform Modifier (Twist/Bend/Taper/Stretch)](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/simple_deform.html) · [Blender 4 Cast Modifier (Sphere/Cylinder/Cuboid)](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/cast.html) · [Blender 4 Wave Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/wave.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [Blender Simple Deform Overview](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_intro.png) · [Blender Twist](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_twist.png) · [Blender Bend](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_bend.png) · [Blender Cast](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_cast_example.jpg) · [Blender Circular Wave](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_wave_example-circular.jpg)
  - **Example (Phase 120):** Select 2+ `TCastleTransform` nodes in the 3D Viewport and press **`Ctrl+Alt+2`** or call `RadialFalloffSelected` (`Click` for default deformation, `Ctrl`-click for secondary preset/normal tilt, `Shift`-click for the interactive parameter dialog).

### Phase 121: 3D Hypocycloid / Epicycloid (Deltoid, Astroid, Nephroid, Ranunculoid) Star Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedInCycloid` / `Ctrl+Alt+4`)
- [x] **121.1. Parametric $k$-Cusped Hypocycloid & Epicycloid Star Arrangement (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInCycloid`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInCycloid(Radius, Cusps, Epicycloid, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms along a $k$-cusped Hypocycloid (`Epicycloid = false`: $x(t) = C_x + \frac{R}{k}((k-1)\cos t + \cos((k-1)t)), \; z(t) = C_z + \frac{R}{k}((k-1)\sin t - \sin((k-1)t))$, e.g., Deltoid $k=3$, Astroid $k=4$) or $k$-cusped Epicycloid (`Epicycloid = true`: $x(t) = C_x + \frac{R}{k+2}((k+1)\cos t - \cos((k+1)t)), \; z(t) = C_z + \frac{R}{k+2}((k+1)\sin t - \sin((k+1)t))$, e.g., Nephroid $k=2$, Ranunculoid $k=5$) in the horizontal XZ plane around $\mathbf{C}$, with `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = analytical cycloid tangent with cusp-singularity fallback, `2` = outward horizontal radial).
  - Added **`Cycloid Star...`** (`ButtonArrangeCycloid` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges in a 4-cusped Astroid Hypocycloid; `Ctrl`-click arranges in a 5-cusped Ranunculoid Epicycloid; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeInCycloidDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+4`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.

### Phase 124: 3D Cylindrical / Capsule (Pill) Shell Deformation (`CylindrifySelected` / `Ctrl+Alt+5`)
- [x] **124.1. Vertical Cylinder & Hemispherical-Cap Capsule Shell Deformation (`TCastleTransformManipulate.CylindrifySelected`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.CylindrifySelected(TargetRadius, StrengthFactor, CapsuleHemispheres, OrientRadially): Boolean`: projects/blends 2+ top-level selected transforms by `StrengthFactor` ($\in [0, 1]$) toward a vertical Cylinder (`CapsuleHemispheres = false`) or a 3D Capsule / Pill shell with hemispherical top/bottom end-caps (`CapsuleHemispheres = true`, clamping spine elevation $Y_{\text{spine},i} \in [C_y - H_{\text{half}}, C_y + H_{\text{half}}]$ and projecting onto a radius-$R$ sphere around $(C_x, Y_{\text{spine},i}, C_z)$), with optional outward surface-normal orientation (`OrientRadially = true`).
  - Added **`Cylindrify / Capsule...`** (`ButtonCylindrifySelection` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click projects 100% onto a vertical cylinder with outward normals; `Ctrl`-click projects 100% onto a 3D capsule with hemispherical end-caps; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowCylindrifySelectionDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+5`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 124):** [CGE Visual Editor Manual](https://castle-engine.io/manual_editor.php) · [CGE Viewport & Scenes](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Blender 4 3D Snapping & Gizmos](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/controls/snapping.html) · [Unity Positioning GameObjects](https://docs.unity3d.com/Manual/PositioningGameObjects.html)

### Phase 125: 3D Cassini Oval / Pinched Peanut / Biconcave Discoid Loop Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedInCassiniOval` / `Ctrl+Alt+9`)
- [x] **125.1. Parametric Cassini Oval / Pinched Peanut & Biconcave Rim Loop (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInCassiniOval`)**

  - Added **`Cassini Oval...`** (`ButtonArrangeCassini` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges in a pinched peanut Cassini oval with tangent orientation; `Ctrl`-click orients inward toward the center; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeInCassiniOvalDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+9`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.

### Phase 126: 3D Catenoid Minimal Surface / Soap-Film Waist Shell Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedOnCatenoid` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+C`)
- [x] **126.1. Golden-Angle Catenoid Minimal-Surface Shell Arrangement (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnCatenoid`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnCatenoid(WaistRadius, HeightSpan, NeckCurvature, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms across a 3D Catenoid Minimal Surface ($u_i = -1 + \frac{2i}{N-1}, \; y_i = C_y + \frac{H}{2}u_i, \; r_i = R_{\text{waist}}\cosh(\kappa u_i), \; \theta_i = i\phi_{\text{golden}}$) with analytical catenary slope $\frac{dr}{dy} = \frac{2\kappa R_{\text{waist}}}{H}\sinh(\kappa u_i)$, supporting `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = outward catenoid minimal-surface normal, `2` = catenary meridian tangent).
  - Added **`Catenoid Shell...`** (`ButtonArrangeCatenoid` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges on a catenoid minimal surface with outward normals; `Ctrl`-click orients along the catenary meridian profile tangent; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeOnCatenoidDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+C`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.

### Phase 128: 3D Barrel / Pincushion / Hourglass Waist Deformation (`BarrelPincushionSelected` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+B`)
- [x] **128.1. Quadratic Longitudinal Barrel Bulge & Pincushion / Hourglass Waist Deformation (`TCastleTransformManipulate.BarrelPincushionSelected`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.BarrelPincushionSelected(Axis, BulgeFactor, ScaleObjects): Boolean`: deforms 2+ top-level selected transforms around the active selection pivot $\mathbf{C}$ along longitudinal `Axis` (`0` = X, `1` = Y, `2` = Z) with a smooth quadratic radial profile $s(u_i) = \max(0.05, 1 + \text{BulgeFactor}\cdot(1 - u_i^2))$ where $u_i \in [-1, +1]$ is normalized longitudinal position (`BulgeFactor > 0` expands the middle into a convex barrel bulge while keeping the $\pm 1$ ends anchored; `BulgeFactor < 0` pinches the middle into a concave hourglass waist), optionally scaling each transform's two transverse local `Scale` components by $s(u_i)$ (`ScaleObjects = true`).
  - Added **`Barrel / Pincushion...`** (`ButtonBarrelPincushion` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click applies $+0.5$ barrel bulge along Y with transverse object scaling; `Ctrl`-click applies $-0.45$ pincushion hourglass waist along Y; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowBarrelPincushionSelectionDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+B`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 128):** [CGE Transform Manipulation](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Blender 4 Simple Deform Modifier (Twist/Bend/Taper/Stretch)](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/simple_deform.html) · [Blender 4 Cast Modifier (Sphere/Cylinder/Cuboid)](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/cast.html) · [Blender 4 Wave Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/wave.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [Blender Simple Deform Overview](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_intro.png) · [Blender Twist](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_twist.png) · [Blender Bend](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_bend.png) · [Blender Cast](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_cast_example.jpg) · [Blender Circular Wave](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_wave_example-circular.jpg)
  - **Example (Phase 128):** Select 2+ `TCastleTransform` nodes in the 3D Viewport and press **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+B`** or call `BarrelPincushionSelected` (`Click` for default deformation, `Ctrl`-click for secondary preset/normal tilt, `Shift`-click for the interactive parameter dialog).

### Phase 129: 3D Hippopede / Spherical Tennis-Ball Seam Curve Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedInHippopede` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+P`)
- [x] **129.1. Parametric Spherical Hippopede / Tennis-Ball Seam Curve (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInHippopede`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInHippopede(Radius, PinchLambda, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms along a 3D Spherical Hippopede / Tennis-Ball Seam Curve on the sphere of radius $R$ centered at $\mathbf{C}$ with $x(t) = C_x + R\left((1-\lambda)\cos t + \lambda\cos 3t\right), \; z(t) = C_z + R\left((1-\lambda)\sin t - \lambda\sin 3t\right), \; y(t) = C_y + 2R\sqrt{\lambda(1-\lambda)}\sin 2t$ ($\lambda \in [0.01, 0.99]$; $\lambda = 0.5$ yields Viviani's spherical figure-8, $\lambda = 0.64$ yields the classic 4-lobed tennis-ball seam), supporting `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = outward sphere normal, `2` = analytical 3D curve tangent).
  - Added **`Hippopede Seam...`** (`ButtonArrangeHippopede` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges on a $\lambda = 0.64$ tennis-ball seam with outward sphere normals; `Ctrl`-click orients along the 3D seam tangent; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeInHippopedeDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+P`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 129):** [CGE 3D Transforms (`TCastleTransform`)](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Blender 4 Array Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/generate/array.html) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Godot 4 Using 3D Transforms](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/3d/using_transforms.html)

  - **Example (Phase 129):** Select 2+ `TCastleTransform` instances and press **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+P`** or invoke `ArrangeSelectedInHippopede` from `PanelLayoutTransform` (`Click` for instant parametric placement, `Ctrl`-click to align local `+Y`/`+Z` with surface normals or curve tangents, `Shift`-click for the modal parameter dialog).

### Phase 132: 3D Directional Sinusoidal Wave / Flag / Snake Deformation (`WaveDeformSelected` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+W`)
- [x] **132.1. Directional Traveling Sinusoidal Wave / Flag / Snake Deformation (`TCastleTransformManipulate.WaveDeformSelected`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.WaveDeformSelected(WaveAxis, DisplaceAxis, Amplitude, Wavelength, PhaseRad, OrientNormals): Boolean`: deforms 2+ top-level selected transforms around the active selection pivot $\mathbf{C}$ by displacing them along `DisplaceAxis` (`0` = X, `1` = Y, `2` = Z) as a sinusoidal function of their signed coordinate $s_i = P_i[\text{WaveAxis}] - C[\text{WaveAxis}]$ along `WaveAxis`: $\Delta d_i = A\sin(k\cdot s_i + \phi_0)$ where $k = \frac{2\pi}{\lambda}$ (`Wavelength <= 0` auto-computes from the selection span along `WaveAxis`), and optionally tilts each transform's world forward (`WaveAxis`) and Up (`DisplaceAxis`) vectors along the analytical wave slope $\frac{d(\Delta d)}{ds} = A k\cos(k\cdot s_i + \phi_0)$ (`OrientNormals = true`).
  - Added **`Wave Deform...`** (`ButtonWaveDeform` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click applies an $X \to Y$ wave with tilted normals; `Ctrl`-click applies a $Z \to Y$ wave with tilted normals; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowWaveDeformSelectionDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+W`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 132):** [CGE Visual Editor Manual](https://castle-engine.io/manual_editor.php) · [CGE Viewport & Scenes](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Blender 4 3D Snapping & Gizmos](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/controls/snapping.html) · [Unity Positioning GameObjects](https://docs.unity3d.com/Manual/PositioningGameObjects.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE 3D Editor](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/fps_editor.webp) · [Blender 4 Snapping Menu](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/editors_3dview_controls_snapping_element-menu.png) · [Unity Handle Position Toggles](https://docs.unity3d.com/uploads/Main/gizmo-handle-position-toggles.png)
  - **Example (Phase 132):** Select target `TCastleTransform` component(s) in `castle-editor` and press **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+W`** or call `WaveDeformSelected` from `PanelLayoutTransform` to apply the transformation as a single Undo-able step.

### Phase 133: 3D Enneper Minimal Surface Saddle Shell Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedOnEnneperSurface` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+E`)
- [x] **133.1. Parametric Higher-Order Enneper Minimal-Surface Saddle Shell (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnEnneperSurface`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnEnneperSurface(ScaleRadius, MaxR, OrderK, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms across a 3D Enneper Minimal Surface of symmetry order $k \in \{1..4\}$ centered at $\mathbf{C}$ in polar coordinates $(r_i, \theta_i)$ with $r_i = R_{\text{max}}\sqrt{u_i}$ and $\theta_i = 2\pi u_i$:
    $$x = C_x + S\left(r\cos\theta - \frac{r^{2k+1}}{2k+1}\cos((2k+1)\theta)\right), \quad z = C_z + S\left(-r\sin\theta - \frac{r^{2k+1}}{2k+1}\sin((2k+1)\theta)\right), \quad y = C_y + S\left(\frac{2r^{k+1}}{k+1}\cos((k+1)\theta)\right)$$
    supporting `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = exact analytical Weierstrass–Enneper Gauss-map surface normal $\mathbf{N} = \frac{1}{1 + r^{2k}}\left(2r^k\cos(k\theta), \; 1 - r^{2k}, \; 2r^k\sin(k\theta)\right)$, `2` = outward radial from $\mathbf{C}$).
  - Added **`Enneper Saddle...`** (`ButtonArrangeEnneper` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges on a classic $k=1$ Enneper saddle shell with Gauss-map surface normals; `Ctrl`-click arranges on a $k=2$ 6-fold monkey-saddle Enneper shell; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeOnEnneperSurfaceDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+E`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 133):** [CGE Transform Manipulation](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Blender 4 Simple Deform Modifier (Twist/Bend/Taper/Stretch)](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/simple_deform.html) · [Blender 4 Cast Modifier (Sphere/Cylinder/Cuboid)](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/cast.html) · [Blender 4 Wave Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/wave.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [Blender Simple Deform Overview](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_intro.png) · [Blender Twist](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_twist.png) · [Blender Bend](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_bend.png) · [Blender Cast](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_cast_example.jpg) · [Blender Circular Wave](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_wave_example-circular.jpg)
  - **Example (Phase 133):** Select 2+ `TCastleTransform` nodes in the 3D Viewport and press **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+E`** or call `ArrangeSelectedOnEnneperSurface` (`Click` for default deformation, `Ctrl`-click for secondary preset/normal tilt, `Shift`-click for the interactive parameter dialog).

### Phase 136: 3D Hyperbolic Paraboloid / Pringle Saddle / Parabolic Dome Curvature Deformation (`SaddleDomeDeformSelected` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+D`)
- [x] **136.1. Bivariate Quadratic Hyperbolic Paraboloid Saddle / Parabolic Dome / Bowl Deformation (`TCastleTransformManipulate.SaddleDomeDeformSelected`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.SaddleDomeDeformSelected(DisplaceAxis, CurvatureU, CurvatureV, OrientNormals): Boolean`: deforms 2+ top-level selected transforms around the active selection pivot $\mathbf{C}$ by displacing them along `DisplaceAxis` (`0` = X, `1` = Y, `2` = Z) according to the normalized bivariate quadratic surface $\Delta d_i = K_u u_i^2 + K_v v_i^2$, where $u_i = \frac{P_i[U] - C[U]}{\text{MaxExt}_U}$ and $v_i = \frac{P_i[V] - C[V]}{\text{MaxExt}_V}$ on the two perpendicular axes $(U, V)$ (`CurvatureU * CurvatureV < 0` yields a hyperbolic paraboloid / Pringle saddle; `CurvatureU, CurvatureV < 0` yields a parabolic dome; `CurvatureU, CurvatureV > 0` yields a parabolic bowl), and optionally tilts each transform's world forward (`AxisU`) and Up (`DisplaceAxis`) vectors along the exact analytical surface normal $\mathbf{N} \propto -s_u\mathbf{e}_U - s_v\mathbf{e}_V + \mathbf{e}_D$ where $s_u = \frac{2K_u u_i}{\text{MaxExt}_U}$ and $s_v = \frac{2K_v v_i}{\text{MaxExt}_V}$ (`OrientNormals = true`).
  - Added **`Saddle / Dome...`** (`ButtonSaddleDomeDeform` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click applies a Hyperbolic Paraboloid Saddle $(+3, -3)$ with tilted normals; `Ctrl`-click applies a Parabolic Dome Canopy $(-3, -3)$ with tilted normals; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowSaddleDomeDeformSelectionDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+D`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 136):** [CGE Transform Manipulation](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Blender 4 Simple Deform Modifier (Twist/Bend/Taper/Stretch)](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/simple_deform.html) · [Blender 4 Cast Modifier (Sphere/Cylinder/Cuboid)](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/cast.html) · [Blender 4 Wave Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/wave.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [Blender Simple Deform Overview](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_intro.png) · [Blender Twist](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_twist.png) · [Blender Bend](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_bend.png) · [Blender Cast](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_cast_example.jpg) · [Blender Circular Wave](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_wave_example-circular.jpg)
  - **Example (Phase 136):** Select 2+ `TCastleTransform` nodes in the 3D Viewport and press **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+D`** or call `SaddleDomeDeformSelected` (`Click` for default deformation, `Ctrl`-click for secondary preset/normal tilt, `Shift`-click for the interactive parameter dialog).

### Phase 140: 3D Octahedral / Superquadric $\ell_p$-Norm Shell Projection & Deformation (`OctahedralDeformSelected` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+Q`)
- [x] **140.1. Parametric $\ell_p$-Norm Octahedral / Star Astroid / Rounded Box Shell Projection (`TCastleTransformManipulate.OctahedralDeformSelected`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.OctahedralDeformSelected(Radius, ExponentP, Strength, OrientNormals): Boolean`: projects or blends 2+ top-level selected transforms around the active selection pivot $\mathbf{C}$ onto a 3D $\ell_p$-norm superquadric shell of radius $R$ and exponent $p = \text{Clamped}(\text{ExponentP}, 0.25, 16.0)$ (`p = 1.0` = regular octahedron / Manhattan diamond crystal, `p < 1.0` = concave 6-pointed star astroid crystal, `p > 2.0` = rounded cube / super-box):
    $$\|\mathbf{d}\|_p = \left(|d_x|^p + |d_y|^p + |d_z|^p\right)^{1/p}, \quad \mathbf{P}_{\text{shell}} = \mathbf{C} + \frac{R}{\|\mathbf{d}\|_p}\mathbf{d}, \quad \mathbf{P}' = \text{Lerp}(\mathbf{P}, \mathbf{P}_{\text{shell}}, \text{Strength})$$
    and when `OrientNormals = true`, aligns each transform's world forward (`LookDir`) along the exact analytical $\ell_p$-shell outward surface normal $\mathbf{N} \propto \left(\text{sgn}(d_x)|d_x|^{p-1}, \; \text{sgn}(d_y)|d_y|^{p-1}, \; \text{sgn}(d_z)|d_z|^{p-1}\right)$.
  - Added **`Octahedral / Lp...`** (`ButtonOctahedralDeform` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click projects onto an Octahedral Diamond shell ($p = 1.0$) with analytical normals; `Ctrl`-click projects onto a Star Astroid shell ($p = 0.65$); `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowOctahedralDeformSelectionDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+Q`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 140):** [CGE Visual Editor Manual](https://castle-engine.io/manual_editor.php) · [CGE Viewport & Scenes](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Blender 4 3D Snapping & Gizmos](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/controls/snapping.html) · [Unity Positioning GameObjects](https://docs.unity3d.com/Manual/PositioningGameObjects.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE 3D Editor](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/fps_editor.webp) · [Blender 4 Snapping Menu](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/editors_3dview_controls_snapping_element-menu.png) · [Unity Handle Position Toggles](https://docs.unity3d.com/uploads/Main/gizmo-handle-position-toggles.png)
  - **Example (Phase 140):** Select target `TCastleTransform` component(s) in `castle-editor` and press **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+Q`** or call `OctahedralDeformSelected` from `PanelLayoutTransform` to apply the transformation as a single Undo-able step.

### Phase 141: 3D Ulisse Dini Pseudospherical Twisted Horn Surface Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedOnDiniSurface` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+T`)
- [x] **141.1. Parametric Ulisse Dini Constant-Negative-Curvature Pseudospherical Twisted Horn (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnDiniSurface`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnDiniSurface(RadiusA, PitchB, Turns, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms across Ulisse Dini's twisted pseudospherical surface of constant negative Gaussian curvature centered at $\mathbf{C}$ with equatorial radius $a$, helical pitch $b$, and turns $W$ ($u_i = 2\pi W t_i$, $v_i = \frac{\pi}{6} + \frac{\pi}{3}t_i \in [\frac{\pi}{6}, \frac{\pi}{2}]$):
    $$x = C_x + a\cos u_i \sin v_i, \quad z = C_z + a\sin u_i \sin v_i, \quad y = C_y + a\left(\cos v_i + \ln\tan\frac{v_i}{2}\right) + b u_i$$
    supporting `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = analytical spiral tangent, `2` = outward horizontal radial from the central Y axis).
  - Added **`Dini Horn...`** (`ButtonArrangeDini` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges on a 2-turn Dini twisted horn along the spiral tangent; `Ctrl`-click arranges on a 3-turn Dini horn facing outward; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeOnDiniSurfaceDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+T`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.

### Phase 142: 3D Euler / Cornu Clothoid S-Curve Spiral Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedInCornuSpiral` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+L`)
- [x] **142.1. Parametric Fresnel-Integral Euler / Cornu Clothoid S-Curve Spiral (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInCornuSpiral`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInCornuSpiral(ScaleLength, MaxParamT, HeightSpan, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms along a 3D Euler / Cornu clothoid S-curve spiral centered at $\mathbf{C}$ whose curvature varies linearly with arc parameter $s_i = T_{\max}(2t_i - 1) \in [-T_{\max}, +T_{\max}]$ via 64-step composite Simpson quadrature of the normalized Fresnel integrals:
    $$C(s) = \int_0^s \cos\left(\frac{\pi}{2}u^2\right)du, \quad S(s) = \int_0^s \sin\left(\frac{\pi}{2}u^2\right)du, \quad \mathbf{P}_i = \mathbf{C} + \left(A \cdot C(s_i), \; H(t_i - 0.5), \; A \cdot S(s_i)\right)$$
    supporting `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = exact analytical clothoid tangent $\mathbf{T}(s_i) \propto \left(A\cos(\frac{\pi}{2}s_i^2), \; \frac{H}{2T_{\max}}, \; A\sin(\frac{\pi}{2}s_i^2)\right)$, `2` = outward radial from $\mathbf{C}$).
  - Added **`Cornu Clothoid...`** (`ButtonArrangeCornu` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges in a planar Cornu clothoid S-curve oriented along the analytical tangent; `Ctrl`-click arranges in a 3D rising Cornu clothoid ramp; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeInCornuSpiralDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+L`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - Added automated unit test `TestOctahedralDeformDiniAndCornuSpiral` in `tests/code/testcases/testcastletransformmanipulate.pas`.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 142):** [CGE 3D Transforms (`TCastleTransform`)](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Blender 4 Array Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/generate/array.html) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Godot 4 Using 3D Transforms](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/3d/using_transforms.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE 3D Transform Scene](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/transform_scene.webp) · [Godot 4 3D Viewport](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/_images/tuto_3d3.webp) · [Blender 4 3D Gizmo](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/editors_3dview_display_gizmo_options-translate.png)
  - **Example (Phase 142):** Select 2+ `TCastleTransform` instances and press **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+L`** or invoke `ArrangeSelectedInCornuSpiral` from `PanelLayoutTransform` (`Click` for instant parametric placement, `Ctrl`-click to align local `+Y`/`+Z` with surface normals or curve tangents, `Shift`-click for the modal parameter dialog).

### Phase 144: 3D Triply Periodic Minimal Surface (Schoen Gyroid / Schwarz P Lattice Wave) Deformation (`GyroidDeformSelected` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+M`)
- [x] **144.1. Parametric Schoen Gyroid & Schwarz P Triply Periodic Minimal Surface (TPMS) Lattice Deformation (`TCastleTransformManipulate.GyroidDeformSelected`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.GyroidDeformSelected(Wavelength, Amplitude, SchwarzPMode, OrientNormals): Boolean`: deforms 2+ top-level selected transforms around the active selection pivot $\mathbf{C}$ by evaluating either Alan Schoen's Gyroid minimal surface ($F(x,y,z) = \sin(kx)\cos(ky) + \sin(ky)\cos(kz) + \sin(kz)\cos(kx)$ when `SchwarzPMode = false`) or Hermann Schwarz's Primitive (Schwarz P) minimal lattice ($F(x,y,z) = \cos(kx) + \cos(ky) + \cos(kz)$ when `SchwarzPMode = true`) with spatial wavenumber $k = \frac{2\pi}{\lambda}$, displacing each transform along the normalized analytical TPMS iso-surface gradient $\hat{\mathbf{g}} = \frac{\nabla F}{\|\nabla F\|}$ by $\Delta \mathbf{P}_i = A \cdot F(\mathbf{d}_i) \cdot \hat{\mathbf{g}}(\mathbf{d}_i)$, and optionally aligning each transform's world forward (`LookDir`) with $\hat{\mathbf{g}}(\mathbf{d}_i)$ (`OrientNormals = true`).
  - Added **`Gyroid / TPMS...`** (`ButtonGyroidDeform` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click deforms along a Schoen Gyroid minimal surface with analytical gradient normals; `Ctrl`-click deforms along a Schwarz P lattice wave; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowGyroidDeformSelectionDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+M`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.

  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ScherkDeformSelected(Wavelength, Amplitude, SaddleTowerMode, OrientNormals): Boolean`: deforms 2+ top-level selected transforms around the active selection pivot $\mathbf{C}$ by displacing their vertical $Y$ coordinate according to Heinrich Scherk's First Doubly-Periodic Minimal Surface ($F(u, v) = \ln\left(\frac{|\cos v|}{|\cos u|}\right)$ with smooth asymptote clamping $\ge 0.18$ when `SaddleTowerMode = false`) or Scherk's Second Saddle-Tower Surface ($F(u, v) = \sin u \sin v$ when `SaddleTowerMode = true`) with spatial wavenumber $k = \frac{2\pi}{\lambda}$, and optionally aligns each transform's $+Y$ Up vector to the exact analytical Scherk surface normal $\mathbf{N} = \text{Normalize}\!\left(-\frac{\partial \Delta y}{\partial x}, \; 1, \; -\frac{\partial \Delta y}{\partial z}\right)$ (`OrientNormals = true`).
  - Added **`Scherk Surface...`** (`ButtonScherkDeform` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click deforms along Scherk's First Minimal Surface with analytical normals; `Ctrl`-click deforms along Scherk's Saddle Tower; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowScherkDeformSelectionDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+J`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.

### Phase 149: 3D Werner Boy Projective Surface / 3-Fold Immersion Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedOnBoysSurface` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+X`)
- [x] **149.1. Parametric Bryant–Kusner 3-Fold Symmetric Werner Boy Immersion of $\mathbb{RP}^2$ (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnBoysSurface`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnBoysSurface(Radius, Turns, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms across the Bryant–Kusner parameterization of Werner Boy's 3-fold symmetric non-orientable projective surface centered at $\mathbf{C}$ over the complex unit disk $w = r_i e^{i\theta_i}$ ($r_i = \sqrt{t_i}, \; \theta_i = 2\pi W t_i$):
    $$g_1 = -\frac{3}{2}\operatorname{Im}\!\left(\frac{w(1 - w^4)}{D}\right), \quad g_2 = -\frac{3}{2}\operatorname{Re}\!\left(\frac{w(1 + w^4)}{D}\right), \quad g_3 = \operatorname{Im}\!\left(\frac{1 + w^6}{D}\right) - \frac{1}{2}, \quad D = w^6 + \sqrt{5}w^3 - 1$$
    mapped via $\mathbf{P}_i = \mathbf{C} + \frac{R}{g_1^2 + g_2^2 + g_3^2}(g_1, \; -g_3, \; g_2)$, supporting `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = analytical spiral tangent, `2` = outward radial from $\mathbf{C}$).
  - Added **`Boy's Surface...`** (`ButtonArrangeBoys` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges on a 2-turn Boy's surface along the tangent; `Ctrl`-click arranges on a 3-turn Boy's surface facing outward; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeOnBoysSurfaceDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+X`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 149):** [CGE 3D Transforms (`TCastleTransform`)](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Blender 4 Array Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/generate/array.html) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Godot 4 Using 3D Transforms](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/3d/using_transforms.html)

### Phase 152: 3D Spherical Stereographic / Inverse Conformal Projection Deformation (`StereographicDeformSelected` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+'`)
- [x] **152.1. Parametric Inverse Stereographic Conformal Sphere Projection (`TCastleTransformManipulate.StereographicDeformSelected`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.StereographicDeformSelected(Radius, Strength, NorthPoleProjection, OrientNormals): Boolean`: deforms 2+ top-level selected transforms around the active selection pivot $\mathbf{C}$ by mapping their horizontal planar offsets $u = \frac{x - C_x}{R}, \; w = \frac{z - C_z}{R}$ ($r^2 = u^2 + w^2$) onto a sphere of radius $R$ via inverse stereographic conformal projection:
    $$\mathbf{S}(u, w) = \mathbf{C} + \frac{R}{r^2 + 1}\Big(2u, \; \pm(r^2 - 1), \; 2w\Big) + (0, \; y - C_y, \; 0)$$
    (using $+1$ for South-Pole projection where origin maps to South Pole $(0, -R, 0)$ when `NorthPoleProjection = false`, or $-1$ for North-Pole dome projection where origin maps to North Pole $(0, +R, 0)$ when `NorthPoleProjection = true`), blended by `Strength` $\in [0, 1]$, and optionally aligns each transform's orientation to the exact conformal spherical normal $\mathbf{N} = \frac{1}{r^2 + 1}(2u, \; \pm(r^2 - 1), \; 2w)$ (`OrientNormals = true`).
  - Added **`Stereographic...`** (`ButtonStereographicDeform` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click applies 100% South-Pole inverse stereographic projection with conformal normals; `Ctrl`-click applies North-Pole dome projection; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowStereographicDeformSelectionDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+'`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 152):** [CGE 3D Transforms (`TCastleTransform`)](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Blender 4 Array Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/generate/array.html) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Godot 4 Using 3D Transforms](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/3d/using_transforms.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE 3D Transform Scene](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/transform_scene.webp) · [Godot 4 3D Viewport](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/_images/tuto_3d3.webp) · [Blender 4 3D Gizmo](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/editors_3dview_display_gizmo_options-translate.png)
  - **Example (Phase 152):** Select 2+ `TCastleTransform` instances and press **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+'`** or invoke `StereographicDeformSelected` from `PanelLayoutTransform` (`Click` for instant parametric placement, `Ctrl`-click to align local `+Y`/`+Z` with surface normals or curve tangents, `Shift`-click for the modal parameter dialog).

    with exact analytical tangent derivatives $\left(R'(\theta)\cos\theta - R(\theta)\sin\theta, \; n A_y\cos(n\theta), \; R'(\theta)\sin\theta + R(\theta)\cos\theta\right)$ and `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = analytical 3D crown tangent, `2` = outward radial from $\mathbf{C}$).

    supporting `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = Dupin cyclide curve tangent, `2` = Dupin cyclide surface normal $\mathbf{P}_v \times \mathbf{P}_u$).

### Phase 157: 3D Edmond Bour Minimal Surface Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedOnBourSurface` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+.`)
- [x] **157.1. Parametric Edmond Bour Algebraic Minimal Surface in Polar Coordinates (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnBourSurface`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnBourSurface(ScaleRadius, Turns, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms across Edmond Bour's 1862 algebraic minimal surface in polar Weierstrass–Enneper coordinates $(r_i, \theta_i)$ ($r_i = 0.25 + 0.75 t_i \in [0.25, 1.0], \; \theta_i = 2\pi W t_i$) centered at $\mathbf{C}$ with scale radius $S$:
    $$x = C_x + S\!\left(r_i\cos\theta_i - \frac{1}{2}r_i^2\cos(2\theta_i)\right), \quad y = C_y + S\!\left(\frac{4}{3}r_i^{3/2}\cos\!\left(\frac{3\theta_i}{2}\right)\right), \quad z = C_z + S\!\left(-r_i\sin\theta_i - \frac{1}{2}r_i^2\sin(2\theta_i)\right)$$
    supporting `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = Bour trajectory tangent, `2` = exact unit Weierstrass–Enneper Gauss-map normal $\mathbf{N}(r_i, \theta_i) = \frac{1}{1 + r_i}\!\left(2\sqrt{r_i}\cos\frac{\theta_i}{2}, \; r_i - 1, \; -2\sqrt{r_i}\sin\frac{\theta_i}{2}\right)$).
  - Added **`Arrange Bour Surface`** (`ButtonArrangeBour` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges on a 2-turn Bour minimal surface along the tangent; `Ctrl`-click arranges on a 3-turn Bour minimal surface aligned to the exact Weierstrass–Enneper normal; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeOnBourSurfaceDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+.`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 157):** [CGE 3D Transforms (`TCastleTransform`)](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Blender 4 Array Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/generate/array.html) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Godot 4 Using 3D Transforms](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/3d/using_transforms.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE 3D Transform Scene](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/transform_scene.webp) · [Godot 4 3D Viewport](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/_images/tuto_3d3.webp) · [Blender 4 3D Gizmo](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/editors_3dview_display_gizmo_options-translate.png)
  - **Example (Phase 157):** Select 2+ `TCastleTransform` instances and press **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+.`** or invoke `ArrangeSelectedOnBourSurface` from `PanelLayoutTransform` (`Click` for instant parametric placement, `Ctrl`-click to align local `+Y`/`+Z` with surface normals or curve tangents, `Shift`-click for the modal parameter dialog).

### Phase 158: 3D Pascal Limaçon / Trisectrix Inner-Loop Curve Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedInLimaconCurve` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+/`)
- [x] **158.1. Parametric Étienne Pascal Limaçon / Trisectrix Inner-Loop Curve (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInLimaconCurve`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInLimaconCurve(LoopRadiusA, OffsetB, CrestHeight, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms around the active selection pivot $\mathbf{C}$ at $\theta_i = \frac{2\pi i}{N}$ along Étienne Pascal's Limaçon $r(\theta_i) = B + A\cos\theta_i$ (defaulting to $B = 0.5 A$ for the self-intersecting Trisectrix inner-loop limaçon when $B \le 0$) with vertical 3D crest modulation $Y = H\sin(2\theta_i)$:
    $$x = C_x + (B + A\cos\theta_i)\cos\theta_i, \quad y = C_y + H\sin(2\theta_i), \quad z = C_z + (B + A\cos\theta_i)\sin\theta_i$$
    with exact analytical tangent derivatives $\left(-B\sin\theta_i - A\sin(2\theta_i), \; 2H\cos(2\theta_i), \; B\cos\theta_i + A\cos(2\theta_i)\right)$ and `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = analytical limaçon tangent, `2` = outward radial from $\mathbf{C}$).
  - Added **`Arrange Limacon Loop`** (`ButtonArrangeLimacon` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges in a Trisectrix inner-loop limaçon along the tangent; `Ctrl`-click arranges in a dimpled convex limaçon with 3D vertical crest; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeInLimaconCurveDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+/`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - Added automated unit test `TestDupinCyclideBourSurfaceAndLimacon` in `tests/code/testcases/testcastletransformmanipulate.pas`.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 158):** [CGE Visual Editor Manual](https://castle-engine.io/manual_editor.php) · [CGE Viewport & Scenes](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Blender 4 3D Snapping & Gizmos](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/controls/snapping.html) · [Unity Positioning GameObjects](https://docs.unity3d.com/Manual/PositioningGameObjects.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE 3D Editor](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/fps_editor.webp) · [Blender 4 Snapping Menu](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/editors_3dview_controls_snapping_element-menu.png) · [Unity Handle Position Toggles](https://docs.unity3d.com/uploads/Main/gizmo-handle-position-toggles.png)
  - **Example (Phase 158):** Select target `TCastleTransform` component(s) in `castle-editor` and press **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+/`** or call `ArrangeSelectedInLimaconCurve` from `PanelLayoutTransform` to apply the transformation as a single Undo-able step.

### Phase 159: Hierarchy Audit: Select Negative-Scale / Mirrored-Determinant Transforms (`SelectNegativeScaleTransforms` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+-`) & 2D UI Split-Screen 2P/3P/4P Viewport Layout (`ArrangeSelectedUserInterfacesInSplitScreen` / `ButtonUISplitScreen`)
- [x] **159.1. Hierarchy Scale & Winding Audit: Select Negative-Scale / Mirrored-Determinant Transforms (`SelectNegativeScaleTransforms` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+-`)**
  - Implemented `TDesignFrame.SelectNegativeScaleTransforms(OddMirroredOnly)`: audits all visible, unlocked `TCastleTransform` components in the hierarchy and selects transforms that have any non-positive scale axis (`S.X <= 0`, `S.Y <= 0`, or `S.Z <= 0`) when `OddMirroredOnly = false`, or strictly negative scale determinant (`S.X * S.Y * S.Z < 0`, which flips triangle winding and face normals inside-out and breaks physics colliders) when `OddMirroredOnly = true`.
  - Bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+-`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree, plus **`Select Negative-Scale / Mirrored-Determinant Transforms (Ctrl+Alt+Shift+-)`** (`MenuTreeViewItemSelectNegativeScale`) in `MenuTreeView` (`Shift`-click selects negative-determinant mirrored transforms only).
- [x] **159.2. 2D UI Split-Screen 2P/3P/4P Viewport Layout (`ArrangeSelectedUserInterfacesInSplitScreen` / `ButtonUISplitScreen`)**
  - Implemented `TDesignFrame.ArrangeSelectedUserInterfacesInSplitScreen(MarginPx, GapPx, VerticalTwoPlayer)` and added **`UI Split-Screen`** (`ButtonUISplitScreen`) to `PanelLayoutUI`, subdividing 2+ selected `TCastleUserInterface` controls or viewports inside their parent container into standard multiplayer split-screen layouts: 2 items in Top/Bottom horizontal split (or Left/Right vertical split when `VerticalTwoPlayer = true`), 3 items in 1 full-width Top + 2 half-width Bottom viewports (or 1 full-height Left + 2 stacked Right viewports when `VerticalTwoPlayer = true`), and 4+ items in a $\lceil\sqrt{N}\rceil \times \lceil N/\text{Cols}\rceil$ quad/grid split-screen (**Click**: $8\text{ px}$ margin/gap horizontal split; **`Shift`-click**: $0\text{ px}$ flush split-screen; **`Ctrl`-click**: Vertical Left/Right 2P/3P split).

### Phase 160: 3D Henneberg Minimal Surface Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedOnHennebergSurface` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+=`)
- [x] **160.1. Parametric Lebrecht Henneberg Non-Orientable Minimal Surface (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnHennebergSurface`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnHennebergSurface(ScaleRadius, Turns, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms across Lebrecht Henneberg's 1875 algebraic non-orientable minimal surface centered at $\mathbf{C}$ with scale radius $S$ and azimuthal turns $W = \text{Turns}$ ($t_i = \frac{i}{N - 1}, \; u_i = 0.8 t_i, \; v_i = 2\pi W t_i$):
    $$x = C_x + S\!\left(2\sinh u_i\cos v_i - \frac{2}{3}\sinh(3u_i)\cos(3v_i)\right), \quad y = C_y + 2S\cosh(2u_i)\cos(2v_i), \quad z = C_z + S\!\left(2\sinh u_i\sin v_i + \frac{2}{3}\sinh(3u_i)\sin(3v_i)\right)$$
    supporting `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = Henneberg trajectory tangent, `2` = Henneberg surface normal $\mathbf{P}_v \times \mathbf{P}_u$).
  - Added **`Arrange Henneberg Surf`** (`ButtonArrangeHenneberg` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges on a 1-turn Henneberg minimal surface along the tangent; `Ctrl`-click arranges on a 2-turn Henneberg surface aligned to the surface normal; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeOnHennebergSurfaceDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+=`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.

### Phase 161: 3D Spherical Epicycloid / Nephroid Seam Curve Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedOnSphericalEpicycloid` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+~`)
- [x] **161.1. Parametric 3D Spherical Epicycloid / Cam-Follower Seam Curve (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnSphericalEpicycloid`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnSphericalEpicycloid(Radius, CuspsK, ConeAngleDeg, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms on a sphere of radius $R$ centered at $\mathbf{C}$ along a $k$-lobe spherical epicycloid / cam seam curve with maximum latitude cone angle $\omega = \text{DegToRad}(\text{ConeAngleDeg})$ ($\theta_i = \frac{2\pi i}{N}, \; \phi_i = \omega\cos(k\theta_i)$):
    $$x = C_x + R\cos\phi_i\cos\theta_i, \quad y = C_y + R\sin\phi_i, \quad z = C_z + R\cos\phi_i\sin\theta_i$$
    with exact analytical seam tangent $\left(-\sin\phi_i\cdot\phi'_i\cos\theta_i - \cos\phi_i\sin\theta_i, \; \cos\phi_i\cdot\phi'_i, \; -\sin\phi_i\cdot\phi'_i\sin\theta_i + \cos\phi_i\cos\theta_i\right)$ and `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = analytical spherical seam tangent, `2` = outward unit sphere normal).
  - Added **`Arrange Sphere Epicycloid`** (`ButtonArrangeSphEpicycloid` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges on a 2-lobe spherical nephroid seam curve along the tangent; `Ctrl`-click arranges on a 3-lobe spherical cam seam curve aligned to the outward sphere normal; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeOnSphericalEpicycloidDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+~`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 161):** [CGE Visual Editor Manual](https://castle-engine.io/manual_editor.php) · [CGE Viewport & Scenes](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Blender 4 3D Snapping & Gizmos](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/controls/snapping.html) · [Unity Positioning GameObjects](https://docs.unity3d.com/Manual/PositioningGameObjects.html)

  - **Example (Phase 161):** Select target `TCastleTransform` component(s) in `castle-editor` and press **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+~`** or call `ArrangeSelectedOnSphericalEpicycloid` from `PanelLayoutTransform` to apply the transformation as a single Undo-able step.

### Phase 162: 3D Talbot / Plateau Lemniscate Curve Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedInTalbotCurve` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+1`)

    $$x = C_x + A\big(1 + F\sin^2\theta_i\big)\cos\theta_i, \quad y = C_y + H\sin(2\theta_i), \quad z = C_z + A\big(1 - F\cos^2\theta_i\big)\sin\theta_i$$

### Phase 164: 3D Theodor Kuen Pseudospherical Surface Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedOnKuenSurface` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+2`)
- [x] **164.1. Parametric Theodor Kuen Constant-Negative-Curvature Pseudospherical Surface (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnKuenSurface`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnKuenSurface(ScaleRadius, Turns, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms across Theodor Kuen's 1884 constant-negative-curvature pseudospherical surface centered at $\mathbf{C}$ with scale radius $S$ and loop turns $W = \text{Turns}$ ($t_i = \frac{i}{N - 1}, \; u_i = 2\pi W(t_i - 0.5), \; v_i = -1.5 + 3.0 t_i, \; \Delta = \cosh^2 v_i + u_i^2\sinh^2 v_i$):
    $$x = C_x + \frac{2S\cosh v_i(\cos u_i + u_i\sin u_i)}{\Delta}, \quad y = C_y + S\!\left(v_i - \frac{\sinh(2v_i)}{\Delta}\right), \quad z = C_z + \frac{2S\cosh v_i(\sin u_i - u_i\cos u_i)}{\Delta}$$
    supporting `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = Kuen trajectory tangent, `2` = Kuen surface normal $\mathbf{P}_v \times \mathbf{P}_u$).

### Phase 165: 3D Herbert Richmond Minimal Surface Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedOnRichmondSurface` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+3`)
- [x] **165.1. Parametric Herbert William Richmond Planar-End Minimal Surface (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnRichmondSurface`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnRichmondSurface(ScaleRadius, OrderM, Turns, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms across Herbert William Richmond's 1904 planar-end minimal surface in polar coordinates $(r_i, \theta_i)$ ($r_i = 0.6 + 0.4 t_i \in [0.6, 1.0], \; \theta_i = 2\pi W t_i$) centered at $\mathbf{C}$ with scale radius $S$ and symmetry order $m = \text{Clamped}(\text{OrderM}, 1, 8)$:
    $$x = C_x + S\!\left(-\frac{\cos\theta_i}{2r_i} - \frac{r_i^{2m+1}}{4m+2}\cos\big((2m+1)\theta_i\big)\right), \quad y = C_y + S\!\left(\frac{r_i^m}{m}\cos(m\theta_i)\right), \quad z = C_z + S\!\left(-\frac{\sin\theta_i}{2r_i} + \frac{r_i^{2m+1}}{4m+2}\sin\big((2m+1)\theta_i\big)\right)$$
    supporting `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = Richmond trajectory tangent, `2` = exact Weierstrass–Enneper Gauss-map normal $\mathbf{N} = \frac{1}{1 + \rho^2}(2\rho\cos\alpha, \; \rho^2 - 1, \; 2\rho\sin\alpha)$ where $\rho = r_i^{m+1}, \; \alpha = (m+1)\theta_i$).
  - Added **`Arrange Richmond Surf`** (`ButtonArrangeRichmond` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges on an order $m=2$ Richmond minimal surface along the tangent; `Ctrl`-click arranges on an order $m=3$ Richmond minimal surface aligned to the exact Weierstrass–Enneper normal; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeOnRichmondSurfaceDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+3`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 165):** [CGE 3D Transforms (`TCastleTransform`)](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Blender 4 Array Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/generate/array.html) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Godot 4 Using 3D Transforms](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/3d/using_transforms.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE 3D Transform Scene](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/transform_scene.webp) · [Godot 4 3D Viewport](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/_images/tuto_3d3.webp) · [Blender 4 3D Gizmo](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/editors_3dview_display_gizmo_options-translate.png)
  - **Example (Phase 165):** Select 2+ `TCastleTransform` instances and press **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+3`** or invoke `ArrangeSelectedOnRichmondSurface` from `PanelLayoutTransform` (`Click` for instant parametric placement, `Ctrl`-click to align local `+Y`/`+Z` with surface normals or curve tangents, `Shift`-click for the modal parameter dialog).

### Phase 166: 3D Devil's Curve / Maltese Cross Lemniscate Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedInDevilsCurve` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+4`)
- [x] **166.1. Parametric Gabriel Cramer Devil's Curve / Maltese Cross 4-Lobe Lemniscate (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInDevilsCurve`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInDevilsCurve(ScaleRadius, LobeRatioK, CrestHeight, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms around the active selection pivot $\mathbf{C}$ at $\theta_i = \frac{2\pi i}{N}$ along Gabriel Cramer's 4-lobe Devil's Curve / Maltese Cross lemniscate $r(\theta_i) = A\sqrt{1 + K\cos(4\theta_i)}$ with vertical 3D crest modulation $Y = H\sin(2\theta_i)$:
    $$x = C_x + r(\theta_i)\cos\theta_i, \quad y = C_y + H\sin(2\theta_i), \quad z = C_z + r(\theta_i)\sin\theta_i$$
    with exact analytical tangent derivatives using $r'(\theta_i) = -\frac{2A^2 K\sin(4\theta_i)}{r(\theta_i)}$ and `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = analytical curve tangent, `2` = outward radial from $\mathbf{C}$).
