### Phase 168: 3D Julius Plücker Ruled Conoid Surface Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedOnPluckerConoid` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+6`)
- [x] **168.1. Parametric Julius Plücker Ruled Conoid Surface (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnPluckerConoid`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnPluckerConoid(ScaleRadius, FoldsN, FoldHeight, Turns, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms across Julius Plücker's 1865 ruled conoid surface centered at $\mathbf{C}$ with scale radius $R$, fold symmetry order $n = \text{Clamped}(\text{FoldsN}, 1, 8)$, fold height $H = \text{FoldHeight}$, and azimuthal turns $W = \text{Turns}$ ($t_i = \frac{i}{N - 1}, \; r_i = R(0.25 + 0.75 t_i), \; \theta_i = 2\pi W t_i$):
    $$x = C_x + r_i\cos\theta_i, \quad y = C_y + H\sin(n\theta_i), \quad z = C_z + r_i\sin\theta_i$$
    supporting `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = Plücker conoid trajectory tangent, `2` = exact ruled conoid surface normal $\mathbf{P}_\theta \times \mathbf{P}_r = (-nH\cos(n\theta_i)\sin\theta_i, \; r_i, \; nH\cos(n\theta_i)\cos\theta_i)$).
  - Added **`Arrange Plucker Conoid`** (`ButtonArrangePlucker` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges on a 2-fold Plücker conoid along the tangent; `Ctrl`-click arranges on a 3-fold Plücker conoid aligned to the exact ruled surface normal; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeOnPluckerConoidDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+6`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 168):** [CGE Visual Editor Manual](https://castle-engine.io/manual_editor.php) · [CGE Viewport & Scenes](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Blender 4 3D Snapping & Gizmos](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/controls/snapping.html) · [Unity Positioning GameObjects](https://docs.unity3d.com/Manual/PositioningGameObjects.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE 3D Editor](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/fps_editor.webp) · [Blender 4 Snapping Menu](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/editors_3dview_controls_snapping_element-menu.png) · [Unity Handle Position Toggles](https://docs.unity3d.com/uploads/Main/gizmo-handle-position-toggles.png)
  - **Example (Phase 168):** Select target `TCastleTransform` component(s) in `castle-editor` and press **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+6`** or call `ArrangeSelectedOnPluckerConoid` from `PanelLayoutTransform` to apply the transformation as a single Undo-able step.

### Phase 169: 3D Alan Barr Superellipsoid / Lamé Surface Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedOnSuperellipsoid` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+7`)
- [x] **169.1. Parametric Alan H. Barr 3D Superellipsoid (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnSuperellipsoid`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedOnSuperellipsoid(Radii, EastWestExp, NorthSouthExp, Turns, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms across Alan H. Barr's 1981 parametric 3D Superellipsoid centered at $\mathbf{C}$ with semi-axes $\mathbf{R} = (R_x, R_y, R_z)$, East-West exponent $e_1 = \text{Clamped}(\text{EastWestExp}, 0.1, 5.0)$, North-South exponent $e_2 = \text{Clamped}(\text{NorthSouthExp}, 0.1, 5.0)$, and azimuthal spiral turns $W = \text{Turns}$ ($t_i = \frac{i}{N - 1}, \; \phi_i = \pi(t_i - 0.5), \; \theta_i = 2\pi W t_i, \; C(\alpha, p) = \operatorname{sgn}(\cos\alpha)|\cos\alpha|^p, \; S(\alpha, p) = \operatorname{sgn}(\sin\alpha)|\sin\alpha|^p$):
    $$x = C_x + R_x C(\phi_i, e_2) C(\theta_i, e_1), \quad y = C_y + R_y S(\phi_i, e_2), \quad z = C_z + R_z C(\phi_i, e_2) S(\theta_i, e_1)$$
    supporting `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = exact Barr dual-superellipsoid outward normal $\left(\frac{1}{R_x} C(\phi_i, 2 - e_2) C(\theta_i, 2 - e_1), \; \frac{1}{R_y} S(\phi_i, 2 - e_2), \; \frac{1}{R_z} C(\phi_i, 2 - e_2) S(\theta_i, 2 - e_1)\right)$, `2` = outward radial from $\mathbf{C}$).
  - Added **`Arrange Superellipsoid`** (`ButtonArrangeSuperellipsoid` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges on a rounded-cube superellipsoid $e_1=0.5, e_2=0.5$ aligned to the exact Barr dual normal; `Ctrl`-click arranges on an octahedral star superellipsoid $e_1=1.5, e_2=1.5$; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeOnSuperellipsoidDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+7`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 169):** [CGE 3D Transforms (`TCastleTransform`)](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Blender 4 Array Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/generate/array.html) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Godot 4 Using 3D Transforms](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/3d/using_transforms.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE 3D Transform Scene](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/transform_scene.webp) · [Godot 4 3D Viewport](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/_images/tuto_3d3.webp) · [Blender 4 3D Gizmo](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/editors_3dview_display_gizmo_options-translate.png)
  - **Example (Phase 169):** Select 2+ `TCastleTransform` instances and press **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+7`** or invoke `ArrangeSelectedOnSuperellipsoid` from `PanelLayoutTransform` (`Click` for instant parametric placement, `Ctrl`-click to align local `+Y`/`+Z` with surface normals or curve tangents, `Shift`-click for the modal parameter dialog).

### Phase 170: 3D Camillo-Christophe Gerono Lemniscate / Besace Saddle Curve Arrangement (`ArrangeSelectedInGeronoLemniscate` / `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+8`)
- [x] **170.1. Parametric Gerono Figure-Eight Lemniscate & Besace Saddle Space Curve (`TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInGeronoLemniscate`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleTransformManipulate.ArrangeSelectedInGeronoLemniscate(LobeRadiusA, LobeWidthB, SaddleHeight, OrientMode): Boolean`: distributes 2+ top-level selected transforms around the active selection pivot $\mathbf{C}$ at $\theta_i = \frac{2\pi i}{N}$ along Camillo-Christophe Gerono's 1854 Figure-Eight Lemniscate / Besace Saddle Space Curve with lobe radius $A$, figure-eight width $B$, and parabolic saddle height $H$:
    $$x = C_x + A\cos\theta_i, \quad y = C_y + H\cos(2\theta_i), \quad z = C_z + B\sin\theta_i\cos\theta_i$$
    with exact non-vanishing analytical tangent derivative $\frac{d\mathbf{P}}{d\theta} = (-A\sin\theta_i, \; -2H\sin(2\theta_i), \; B\cos(2\theta_i))$ and `OrientMode` (`0` = keep rotation, `1` = analytical Gerono/Besace tangent, `2` = outward radial from $\mathbf{C}$).
  - Added **`Arrange Gerono Curve`** (`ButtonArrangeGerono` in `PanelLayoutTransform`: normal click arranges in a planar Gerono lemniscate along the tangent; `Ctrl`-click arranges in a 3D Besace parabolic saddle curve $H=3.0$ along the tangent; `Shift`-click opens the custom dialog) and `ShowArrangeInGeronoLemniscateDialog` bound to **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+8`** in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree.
  - Added automated unit test `TestPluckerSuperellipsoidAndGeronoLemniscate` in `tests/code/testcases/testcastletransformmanipulate.pas`.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 170):** [CGE Transform Manipulation](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Blender 4 Simple Deform Modifier (Twist/Bend/Taper/Stretch)](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/simple_deform.html) · [Blender 4 Cast Modifier (Sphere/Cylinder/Cuboid)](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/cast.html) · [Blender 4 Wave Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/deform/wave.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [Blender Simple Deform Overview](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_intro.png) · [Blender Twist](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_twist.png) · [Blender Bend](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_simple-deform_bend.png) · [Blender Cast](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_cast_example.jpg) · [Blender Circular Wave](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/modeling_modifiers_deform_wave_example-circular.jpg)
  - **Example (Phase 170):** Select 2+ `TCastleTransform` nodes in the 3D Viewport and press **`Ctrl+Alt+Shift+8`** or call `ArrangeSelectedInGeronoLemniscate` (`Click` for default deformation, `Ctrl`-click for secondary preset/normal tilt, `Shift`-click for the interactive parameter dialog).

- [x] **180.1. Context-Aware 3D Ray-Hit / Camera-Front & 2D Parent-Center Paste (`TDesignFrame.PasteComponentHere`)**
  - Implemented `TDesignFrame.PasteComponentHere` bound to **`Ctrl+Shift+V`** (in both the Viewport and Hierarchy Tree) and **`Paste Component Here (At 3D Cursor / UI Center) (Ctrl+Shift+V)`** (`MenuTreeViewItemPasteHere` in `MenuTreeView`).
  - Pastes the clipboard component and automatically snaps a pasted `TCastleTransform` onto the 3D surface under the mouse cursor (`SnapSelectedToRay`)—or places it $10\text{ units}$ in front of `InternalCamera` if no surface is hit—and centers a pasted `TCastleUserInterface` inside its parent container (`CenterSelectedUserInterfacesInParent`).

### Phase 183: Unpack / Inline Sub-Design Instance (`UnpackSelectedDesignInstance` / `MenuTreeViewItemUnpackDesign`)
- [x] **183.1. Convert Selected `TCastleTransformDesign` or `TCastleDesign` Instance into an Editable In-Place Hierarchy (`TDesignFrame.UnpackSelectedDesignInstance`)**
  - Implemented `TDesignFrame.UnpackSelectedDesignInstance` and added **`Unpack / Inline Sub-Design Instance`** (`MenuTreeViewItemUnpackDesign`) in `MenuTreeView`.

### Phase 188: Copy & Paste All Published Component Properties (`CopyAllComponentProperties` / `PasteAllComponentProperties` / `MenuTreeViewItemCopyProps` & `MenuTreeViewItemPasteProps`)
- [x] **188.1. Recursive RTTI Published Property Copy & Paste Across 1+ Target Components (`TDesignFrame.CopyAllComponentProperties` / `PasteAllComponentProperties`)**
  - Implemented `TDesignFrame.CopyAllComponentProperties` and `TDesignFrame.PasteAllComponentProperties` (`MenuTreeViewItemCopyProps` and `MenuTreeViewItemPasteProps` in `MenuTreeView`).
  - Serializes the source component (`FCopiedComponentPropsJson`) and recursively copies all matching published RTTI properties (including nested subcomponents such as `TranslationPersistent`, `RotationPersistent`, `ScalePersistent`, `ColorPersistent`, `Border`, and same-design component references while preserving each target's unique `Name`) onto one or more selected target components in a single Undo step.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 188):** [CGE Visual Editor Manual](https://castle-engine.io/manual_editor.php) · [CGE Viewport & Scenes](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Blender 4 3D Snapping & Gizmos](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/controls/snapping.html) · [Unity Positioning GameObjects](https://docs.unity3d.com/Manual/PositioningGameObjects.html)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE 3D Editor](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/fps_editor.webp) · [Blender 4 Snapping Menu](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/editors_3dview_controls_snapping_element-menu.png) · [Unity Handle Position Toggles](https://docs.unity3d.com/uploads/Main/gizmo-handle-position-toggles.png)
  - **Example (Phase 188):** Select target `TCastleTransform` component(s) in `castle-editor` and call `ScalePersistent` from `PanelLayoutTransform` to apply the transformation as a single Undo-able step.

### Phase 192: Flatten / Unparent Selected Components to World / Root (`FlattenSelectedHierarchy` / `MenuTreeViewItemFlattenSelected`)
- [x] **192.1. World-Transform-Preserving Unparenting (`TDesignFrame.FlattenSelectedHierarchy`)**
  - Implemented `TDesignFrame.FlattenSelectedHierarchy` and added **`Flatten / Unparent Selected to World Root`** (`MenuTreeViewItemFlattenSelected`) in `MenuTreeView`.
  - Detaches selected `TCastleTransform` or `TCastleUserInterface` components from nested parent containers and reparents them directly under the top-level world root (`CurrentViewport.Items` or `FDesignRoot`) while preserving their world-space `GetWorldView` / `SetWorldView` and world scale (for 3D transforms) or container-space rectangle via `AdjustUserInterfaceAnchorsToKeepRect` (for 2D UI controls).

### Phase 193: Bake / Freeze Scale into Primitive Geometry & Children (`FreezeSelectedPrimitiveScale` / `ButtonFreezeScale`)
- [x] **193.1. Primitive Geometry & Child Offset Scale Baker (`TDesignFrame.FreezeSelectedPrimitiveScale`)**
  - Added **`Freeze Scale (1,1,1)`** (`ButtonFreezeScale` on `PanelLayoutTransform` and `MenuTreeViewItemFreezeScale` in `MenuTreeView`) and implemented `TDesignFrame.FreezeSelectedPrimitiveScale(PropagateScaleToChildren)`.
  - Bakes local `Scale` into primitive geometry dimensions (`TCastleBox.Size`, `TCastleSphere.Radius`, `TCastleCylinder.Radius`/`Height`, `TCastleCone.BottomRadius`/`Height`, `TCastlePlane.Size`) and direct child `Translation` offsets (plus child `Scale` for groups or when `Shift` is held), resets `Scale` to `Vector3(1, 1, 1)`, and recalculates attached `TCastleCollider.CalculateSize`.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 193):** [CGE Visual Editor Manual](https://castle-engine.io/manual_editor.php) · [CGE Viewport & Scenes](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Blender 4 3D Snapping & Gizmos](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/controls/snapping.html) · [Unity Positioning GameObjects](https://docs.unity3d.com/Manual/PositioningGameObjects.html)

  - **Example (Phase 193):** Select target `TCastleTransform` component(s) in `castle-editor` and call `FreezeSelectedPrimitiveScale` from `PanelLayoutTransform` to apply the transformation as a single Undo-able step.

### Phase 214: Scene Instancing & `TCastleTransformReference` Deduplicator / Unlinker (`ShowSceneInstancingDialog` / `ApplySceneInstancing`)
- [x] **214.1. Duplicate-URL `TCastleScene` to `TCastleTransformReference` Converter & Unlinker (`TDesignFrame.ShowSceneInstancingDialog` / `TDesignFrame.ApplySceneInstancing`)**
  - Implemented `TDesignFrame.ShowSceneInstancingDialog` and `TDesignFrame.ApplySceneInstancing` (`MenuTreeViewItemSceneInstancing` in `MenuTreeView`).
  - Scans the selected subtree or entire design for leaf `TCastleScene` instances sharing the same `Url`, keeps the first as the master scene, and converts duplicates in-place into lightweight `TCastleTransformReference` instances (`rtIgnoreTransform`, `rtIgnoreTranslation`, or `rtDoNotIgnore`), or unlinks `TCastleTransformReference` instances back into independent deep-copied clones.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 214):** [CGE Visual Editor Manual](https://castle-engine.io/manual_editor.php) · [CGE Viewport & Scenes](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [UE 5.5 Transforming Actors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Blender 4 3D Snapping & Gizmos](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/controls/snapping.html) · [Unity Positioning GameObjects](https://docs.unity3d.com/Manual/PositioningGameObjects.html)

  - **Example (Phase 214):** Select target `TCastleTransform` component(s) in `castle-editor` and call `ShowSceneInstancingDialog` from `PanelLayoutTransform` to apply the transformation as a single Undo-able step.

- [x] **219.1. Sub-Millimeter Float Epsilon, Non-Positive Scale, UI Pixel & Empty Node Sanitizer (`TDesignFrame.ShowHierarchySanitizerDialog` / `TDesignFrame.ApplyHierarchySanitizer`)**
  - Implemented `TDesignFrame.ShowHierarchySanitizerDialog` and `TDesignFrame.ApplyHierarchySanitizer` (`MenuTreeViewItemHierarchySanitizer` in `MenuTreeView`).
  - Cleans floating-point drift on 3D `Translation`, `Scale`, and `Rotation` (`0.0001` epsilon), clamps non-positive scales (`<= 0`), rounds 2D UI `Width`, `Height`, `HorizontalAnchorDelta`, `VerticalAnchorDelta`, and `Border.All` to crisp integer pixels, and optionally prunes empty `TCastleTransform` nodes (`Shift`-click runs default cleanup immediately).

### Phase 251: 3D Viewport & Selected Subtree Model Exporter (`glTF` / `X3D` / `STL`) with Configurable `TUrlProcessing` Resource Packaging (`ShowExportModelDialog` / `ExportToModelWithOptions`)
- [x] **251.1. Viewport & Selected `TCastleTransform` Subtree Exporter with 5 `TUrlProcessing` Resource Packaging Modes (`TDesignFrame.ShowExportModelDialog` / `TDesignFrame.ExportToModelWithOptions`)**
  - Resolved the `TODO` in `TDesignFrame.ExportToModel` (`tools/castle-editor/code/framedesign.pas`) and added `MenuTreeViewItemExportModelWizard` under `MenuTreeViewSubmenuBatchTools`.
  - Supports exporting either the **Entire Active 3D Viewport** (`CurrentViewport.InternalBuildNode` including `Items`, `Background`, `Fog`, and `TCastleNavigation` info node) or **Only the Selected `TCastleTransform` Subtree** (`TargetTransform.InternalBuildNode as TAbstractChildNode`, enabling export of kitbashed modular groups, parametric arrangements, procedural `TCastleTerrain`, or `.castle-transform` designs even when no `TCastleViewport` is present).
  - Supports all 5 `TUrlProcessing` resource packaging modes: `suChangeCastleDataToRelative` (default), `suChangeAllPathsToRelative`, `suEmbedResources` (self-contained `data:` URI embedding), `suCopyResourcesToSubdirectory` (`<name>_resources/` bundle), and `suNone`, plus optional **Center / Zero Root Translation at `(0, 0, 0)`** when exporting a selected subtree (`Shift`-click exports a self-contained embedded `suEmbedResources` model immediately).
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 251):** [CGE Saving / Exporting 3D Models (`SaveNode`, `glTF`, `X3D`, `STL`)](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [CGE Network & `data:` URI Embedding](https://castle-engine.io/manual_network.php) · [Blender 4 glTF 2.0 Import/Export](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/addons/import_export/scene_gltf2.html) · [UE 5.5 glTF Exporter](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/unreal-editor-interface)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE 3D Transform Scene](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/transform_scene.webp) · [CGE 3D Viewport Nice View](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_size/viewport_3d_nice_view.webp) · [Godot 4 3D Viewport](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/_images/tuto_3d3.webp)
  - **Example (Phase 251):** Select a kitbashed `TCastleTransform` group or `TCastleTerrain` in the Hierarchy, right-click -> **`Batch, Prefab & Asset Utilities`** -> **`Export 3D Viewport / Selected Subtree to glTF / X3D / STL Model...`** (`ShowExportModelDialog`), choose `Selected TCastleTransform Subtree Only` + `suEmbedResources` (`Embed All Textures & Resources Using data: URIs`) + `Center / Zero Root Translation at (0, 0, 0)`, and save as `fortress_tower.gltf` or `fortress_tower.x3d`.

### Phase 259: Scrollbar-Preserving & Single-Pass `ModifiedOutsideObjectInspector` Refresh (`UpdateSelectedControlModified`), Unified Physics Simulation Action Sync (`UpdateSimulationActions`), and Safe Modal Download Cleanup (`TProgressForm.WaitFor`)

### Phase 260: Sprite Sheet Editor 2D Orthographic Preview Camera Modernization (`ResetPreviewCamera` Replacing Deprecated `MainScene`), In-Place Hierarchy Tree Node Moves for Behaviors & Non-Visual Components (`MoveBehavior` / `MoveNonVisual`), and Top-Level `Viewport` Menu Sync (`MenuItemViewport`)
- [x] **260.1. `TSpriteSheetEditorForm.ResetPreviewCamera`, In-Place `MoveBehavior` / `MoveNonVisual` Hierarchy Tree Updates & `MenuItemViewport` State Sync (`formspritesheeteditor.pas`, `framedesign.pas`, `formproject.pas`)**
  - Resolved the deprecated `FViewport.Items.MainScene := FPreviewScene` `TODO` in `TSpriteSheetEditorForm.CreatePreviewUIIfNeeded` (`tools/castle-editor/code/formspritesheeteditor.pas`) by introducing `TSpriteSheetEditorForm.ResetPreviewCamera(OrthoWidth, OrthoHeight)` and calling `PlayAnimation` before resetting the 2D camera in `LoadAnimationInPreview`. `ResetPreviewCamera` centers the 2D orthographic camera on `FViewport.ItemsBoundingBox.Center.XY`, resets `Orthographic.Origin := (0.5, 0.5)` and `Orthographic.Scale := 1.0`, and sets both `Orthographic.Width` and `Orthographic.Height` so tall or off-center anchored sprite sheets (such as `tentacles.castle-sprite-sheet`) fit proportionally without relying on deprecated `MainScene`.
  - Resolved the `MoveBehavior` and `MoveNonVisual` tree-update `TODO`s in `TDesignFrame.ControlsTreeDragDrop` (`tools/castle-editor/code/framedesign.pas`) so reordering or moving `TCastleBehavior` and non-visual components updates `ControlsTree` nodes in-place via `MoveOnlyTreeNodes` / `SrcNode.MoveTo` + `ValidateHierarchy` (and refreshes collider wireframes via `UpdateColliders` when moving a `TCastleCollider` to another `TCastleTransform`), falling back to `UpdateDesign` only when a `'Behaviors'` or `'Non-Visual Components'` section header node is created or removed.
  - Resolved the `MenuItemViewport.Enabled` `TODO` in `TProjectForm.ActionViewportUpdate` and `TProjectForm.DesignExistenceChanged` (`tools/castle-editor/code/formproject.pas`) so `MenuItemViewport.Enabled` tracks `Design <> nil` when designs open/close while child viewport actions dynamically enable/disable via `ActionViewportUpdate`.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 260):** [CGE Sprite Sheets & Sprite Sheet Editor Manual](https://castle-engine.io/sprite_sheets) · [CGE Visual Editor Manual (Hierarchy Drag-and-Drop, Behaviors & Viewport Menu)](https://castle-engine.io/manual_editor.php) · [CGE Behaviors (`TCastleBehavior`) Manual](https://castle-engine.io/behaviors) · [Godot 4 `AnimatedSprite2D` & SpriteFrames Editor](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/2d/2d_sprite_animation.html) · [UE 5.5 Paper2D Flipbook Editor & Outliner Component Drag](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/paper-2d-flipbooks-in-unreal-engine)

### Phase 261: Container-Aware UI Drag Delegation (`ShouldDelegateUiDragToParent` / `ChangeDraggedUI` with Lock Guard), Unified Shell Tree Sort Refresh (`SetFileSortType`), and Open-Existing-View Column Sort Indicator Sync (`ListOpenExistingViewRefresh`)

### Phase 264: Active OpenGL Context Sharing & Idle FPS Tracking (`TCastleControl`), Lazarus IDE Project Change `castle-data:/` Sync (`TCastleLazarusIDEIntegration`), and `EditorAllowResize` Diagnostics (`TCastleNotifications` / `TCastleTouchControl`)
- [x] **264.1. `TCastleControl.UpdateSharedControl` / `CreateHandle`, `Fps.InternalSleeping`, Client-Coordinate `DoMouseWheel`, `LazarusIDE.AddHandlerOnProjectOpened` & `EditorAllowResize` (`castlecontrol.pas`, `castleinternallcldesign.pas`, `castlenotifications.pas`, `castleviewport_touchnavigation.inc`, `TODO.md`)**
  - Resolved the `SharedControl`, `Fps.InternalSleeping`, and `DoMouseWheel` `TODO`s in `TCastleControl` (`src/lcl/castlecontrol.pas`):
    - Added `TCastleControl.UpdateSharedControl` and `CreateHandle` override so new or re-docked `TCastleControl` instances prioritize sharing their OpenGL context with a control in `ControlsList` that already has `GLInitialized and HandleAllocated` (preventing context-sharing failures when `ControlsList[0]` is not yet active or during docked form layout changes).
    - Called `C.Container.Fps.InternalSleeping` in `TCastleControl.DoUpdateEverything` when `not C.Invalidated` (matching `TCastleWindow`), ensuring `Fps.WasSleeping` and `Fps.ToString` accurately reflect idle frames when `AutoRedisplay = false`.
    - Updated `TCastleControl.DoMouseWheel` to convert `MousePos` via `MousePosToCastle(MousePos.X, MousePos.Y)` when `PtInRect(ClientRect, MousePos)` holds (falling back to `Container.MousePosition`).
  - Resolved the `FixApplicationDataInIDE` `TODO` in `TCastleLazarusIDEIntegration` (`src/lcl/castleinternallcldesign.pas`) by registering `LazarusIDE.AddHandlerOnProjectOpened` and `LazarusIDE.AddHandlerOnProjectClose` in `TCastleLazarusIDEIntegration.Create` (and removing them in `Destroy`) so `ApplicationDataOverride` (`castle-data:/`) automatically tracks the active Lazarus IDE project whenever projects are opened or closed.
  - Implemented `EditorAllowResize` for `TCastleNotifications` (`src/ui/castlenotifications.pas`) and `TCastleTouchNavigation.TCastleTouchControl` (`src/scene/castleviewport_touchnavigation.inc`), providing clear resize-lock explanations in the Visual Designer and completing the item in `tools/castle-editor/TODO.md`.

### Phase 266: Build Output Warning/Error Tab Counters, In-Place Transient Compiler Progress Replacement & `CastleLog` Highlighting (`TOutputList` / `TAsynchronousProcess`), and Content Browser View-Style Switcher (`BtnFilesViewStyle`)
- [x] **266.1. `TOutputList` Warning/Error Counters & Transient Progress Compaction, `CastleLog` / Build Tool Warning/Error Highlighting & `BtnFilesViewStyle` (`editorutils.pas`, `formproject.pas`, `TODO.md`)**
  - Upgraded `TOutputList` (`tools/castle-editor/code/editorutils.pas`) to track `WarningsCount` and `ErrorsCount` with an `OnCountsChanged` callback wired to `TProjectForm.OutputCountsChanged` (`tools/castle-editor/code/formproject.pas`), dynamically updating `TabOutput.Caption` (and `DesignOutputForm.Caption` in docked mode) to show non-zero error and warning counts (e.g. `Output (2 warnings)` or `Output (1 errors, 3 warnings)`).
  - Implemented in-place replacement of transient compiler progress lines (`Compiling ...` and `Writing Resource String Table file: ...`) in `TOutputList.AddLine` (`FReplaceLastProgressLine`) so rapid unit-compilation progress updates serve as a live indicator without cluttering the output history, and updated `TAsynchronousProcessQueue.Update` to report `'Command finished successfully.'` when `ExitStatus = 0`.
  - Expanded `TAsynchronousProcess.Update.LineOutputKind` (`tools/castle-editor/code/editorutils.pas`) to colorize `CastleLog` runtime warnings (`Warning: ...`), build-tool warnings/errors (`castle-engine: Warning: ...` / `castle-engine: Error: ...`), and top-level FPC/Delphi `Error:` / `Fatal:` messages alongside file-line compiler diagnostics.
  - Added `BtnFilesViewStyle` (`Caption := 'View: Details'`, `BtnFilesViewStyleClick`) to `PanelFilesTop` in `TProjectForm.FormCreate.CreateShellViews` (`tools/castle-editor/code/formproject.pas`), cycling `ShellListView1.ViewStyle` across **Details (`vsReport`)**, **List (`vsList`)**, **Large Icons (`vsIcon`)**, and **Small Icons (`vsSmallIcon`)**.

### Phase 267: Dynamic `ListOutput` `ScrollWidth` & Output Word Wrap Toggle (`TOutputList` / `MenuItemOutputWordWrap`), Verbose Build Tool Output Toggle (`MenuItemVerboseBuildTool`), and Interactive Project Options (`CastleEngineManifest.xml`) Dialog (`MenuItemProjectOptions` / `ShowProjectOptionsDialog`)

  - Fixed the spurious horizontal scrollbar on `ListOutput` and `ListWarnings` on Windows (`tools/castle-editor/code/formproject.lfm`) by resetting hardcoded `ScrollWidth = 902` to `ScrollWidth = 0` and tracking `FMaxLineWidth` dynamically in `TOutputList.AddLine` and `TOutputList.Clear` (`tools/castle-editor/code/editorutils.pas`) so `ListOutput.ScrollWidth` is only enabled when an output line actually exceeds `ListOutput.ClientWidth`.
  - Added configurable **Word Wrap Output** (`TOutputList.WordWrap` in `tools/castle-editor/code/editorutils.pas` and `MenuItemOutputWordWrap` in `OutputPopup` in `tools/castle-editor/code/formproject.pas`, persisted via `ProjectForm_OutputWordWrap` in `UserConfig`), wrapping long non-transient compiler/log lines at word or path boundaries while keeping `ScrollWidth = 0` and incrementing warning/error counters once per logical message.

  - **Example (Phase 267):** Open **Project -> Project Options (`CastleEngineManifest.xml`)...** to edit the project's `caption`, `qualified_name` (`com.mycompany.mygame`), `author`, `version` (`value` & `code`), `screen_orientation`, `fullscreen_immersive`, and primary icon path (`icon.png`) with immediate `CastleEngineManifest.xml` persistence; toggle **Run -> Verbose Build Tool Output (`--verbose`)** to inspect full compiler invocations; or right-click the **Output** panel and toggle **Word Wrap Output** to wrap long compiler command lines cleanly without a horizontal scrollbar.

### Phase 268: Shorter Compiler Output (`IsRedundantCompilerBannerLine` & Separator Cleanup), Scene Loading Indicator (`"Wait, loading..."`), External Asset Import to `castle-data:/` with Dependency Tracking (`WarningOrImportIfOutsideDataDirectory`), and DPI-Aware `TNewProjectForm` Sizing (`AdjustFormSize`)

  - Streamlined build tool and compiler output in `TAsynchronousProcess` and `TOutputList` (`tools/castle-editor/code/editorutils.pas`) by filtering redundant FPC logo/copyright/target banner lines (`Free Pascal Compiler version ...`, `Copyright (c) 1993-...`, `Target OS: ...`) via `IsRedundantCompilerBannerLine` when `--verbose` is not active, and removing extra blank separator lines around bold `okImportantInfo` header/footer lines.

### Phase 269: Collapsible Multiline Log Sections (`TMultilineOutputInfo` / `TOutputList.ToggleExpandItem`), Invisible-Grid Snapping for 2D UI Dragging & Resizing (`TDesignerLayer.Motion`), and Keep Parent Selected When `Shift`-Adding Components (`TDesignFrame.AddComponent`)
- [x] **269.1. Collapsible Multiline Log Sections in `ListOutput`, 2D UI Invisible-Grid Snapping & `Shift`-Add Component Parent Selection (`editorutils.pas`, `formproject.pas`, `framedesign.pas`, `TODO.md`)**
  - Implemented collapsible/expandable multiline log sections in `TOutputList` (`TMultilineOutputInfo` and `TOutputList.ToggleExpandItem` in `tools/castle-editor/code/editorutils.pas`, wired to `TProjectForm.ListOutputDblClick` in `tools/castle-editor/code/formproject.pas`), automatically collapsing `WritelnLogMultiline` blocks delimited by `'-------------------- <Category> begin'` and `'-------------------- <Category> end'` (such as `"OpenGL Information"`, `"Sound"`, and shader/build diagnostics) into a single `[+] <Category> (N lines, double-click to expand)` summary row that expands (`[-]`) or collapses (`[+]`) in-place on double-click while preserving warning/error severity counters and source-line jump support on expanded lines.
  - Upgraded 2D UI dragging and resizing in `TDesignerLayer.Motion` (`tools/castle-editor/code/framedesign.pas`) so `SpinEditSnap` snaps the resulting `UI.Translation.X` / `UI.Translation.Y` (during `dmTranslate`) or `UI.Width` / `UI.Height` (during `dmResize`) to exact multiples of `Snap` (`Round(Val / Snap) * Snap`, acting like an invisible Delphi/Lazarus grid across all anchor modes), while holding `Shift` preserves relative increment step snapping.
  - Updated `TDesignFrame.AddComponent` (`FinishAddingComponent` in `tools/castle-editor/code/framedesign.pas`) so holding `Shift` (`ssShift in GetKeyShiftState` or `mkShift in CastleControl.Container.Pressed.Modifiers`) when adding a component keeps `ParentComponent` selected instead of switching selection to the newly added child, enabling rapid insertion of multiple sibling components under the same container.

  - **Example (Phase 269):** Run a project (`F9`) to see multiline log blocks such as **`[+] OpenGL Information (42 lines, double-click to expand)`** collapsed cleanly into a single row in **Output** and double-click the row to expand (`[-]`) or collapse (`[+]`) its lines in-place; set **`2D snap`** (`SpinEditSnap`) to `10` and drag or resize a `TCastleButton` at `(13, 27)` to watch its position/size snap directly onto exact grid multiples `(20, 30)` (or hold `Shift` for relative `+10` steps); or hold `Shift` while clicking **Add User Interface -> Button** to keep the parent `TCastleVerticalGroup` selected and add several sibling buttons in a row.

### Phase 271: `CASTLE_ENGINE_EDITOR_INSIDE=true` & `Cge-output,` Structured Log Protocol + Collapsible Verbose FPC Command Line (`castlelog.pas` / `editorutils.pas`), Enhanced Audio Preview with Loop & Live Playback Time (`frameviewfile.pas` / `formproject.pas`), and Auto-Generate Missing `.lpi`/`.dproj` on Opening Lazarus/Delphi (`ActionOpenProjectCodeExecute`)
- [x] **271.1. `CASTLE_ENGINE_EDITOR_INSIDE=true` & `Cge-output,` Protocol + Collapsible FPC Command Line, Audio Loop/Playback Preview & Auto-Generate Missing IDE Project Files (`castlelog.pas`, `editorutils.pas`, `frameviewfile.pas`, `formproject.pas`, `TODO.md`)**
  - Updated `InitializeLog` in `src/base/castlelog.pas` so `WantsLogToStandardOutput` is enabled whenever `CASTLE_ENGINE_EDITOR_INSIDE=true` (in addition to `CASTLE_LOG=stdout`), and configured `TAsynchronousProcess.Start` (`tools/castle-editor/code/editorutils.pas`) to set `CASTLE_ENGINE_EDITOR_INSIDE=true` and `CASTLE_LOG=stdout` in the child environment so `castle-engine` and user game processes always stream `CastleLog` output directly to `ListOutput` even on Windows GUI targets.
  - Added `Cge-output,` structured tag parsing (`Cge-output,error:`, `Cge-output,warning:`, `Cge-output,important:`, `Cge-output,info:`, `Cge-output,multiline-begin:`, `Cge-output,multiline-end:`) in `TAsynchronousProcess.Update` and `TOutputList.AddLine` (`tools/castle-editor/code/editorutils.pas`), plus automatic collapsing of long verbose FPC command-line invocations (`IsVerboseFpcCommandLine`) into a single expandable `[+] FPC Command Line (N lines, double-click to expand)` item with one argument per expanded row.
  - Enhanced `TViewFileFrame` (`tools/castle-editor/code/frameviewfile.pas`) with a `LOOP: OFF` / `LOOP: ON` toggle button (`SoundLoopButton`), live playback offset/duration display (`Playing: 0.42s / 2.15s`) in `UpdateLabelVolume`, and `TogglePlaySound`, and updated `TProjectForm.ShellListViewDoubleClick` (`tools/castle-editor/code/formproject.pas`) so double-clicking an audio asset opens `castle-audio-player` if installed or immediately toggles in-editor audio playback.
  - Updated `TProjectForm.ActionOpenProjectCodeExecute` (`AutoGenerateProgramIfMissing` in `tools/castle-editor/code/formproject.pas`) to automatically run `castle-engine generate-program` before launching Lazarus or Delphi when the `.lpi` or `.dproj` file does not yet exist on disk, avoiding missing-file errors without overwriting existing `.lpr` files.

### Phase 272: Fix Top-Right / Edge-Anchored 2D UI Resize Stutter (`TDesignerLayer.Motion.DragAllowed`), Smart Timestamp-Preserving Template Extraction & Custom Editor Rebuild (`ExtractTemplateFile` / `DoEditorRebuildIfNeeded`), and Control-Level Button & UI Theme/Color Skin Presets (`ShowNineSliceSetupDialog`)
- [x] **272.1. Edge-Anchored 2D UI Resize Fix, Timestamp-Preserving Custom Editor Rebuild & Control-Level Button/UI Skin Presets (`framedesign.pas`, `toolproject.pas`, `TODO.md`)**
  - Fixed top-right and edge-anchored 2D UI resizing stutter in `TDesignerLayer.Motion.DragAllowed` (`tools/castle-editor/code/framedesign.pas`) by scaling `ScaledMove := Move * UI.UIScale` to match `UI.RenderRectWithBorder`, adding a `0.5 px` sub-pixel parent boundary tolerance (`ParentRenderRect(UI).Grow(0.5)`) to avoid IEEE-754 ULP rounding rejections on the stationary opposite border, and checking only the actively dragged edges (`hpLeft`, `hpRight`, `vpBottom`, `vpTop`) during `dmResize`.
  - Upgraded `TCastleProject.ExtractTemplateFile` and `TCastleProject.DoEditorRebuildIfNeeded` (`tools/build-tool/code/toolproject.pas`) so generated `.lpi`/`.lpk`/`.pas` template files are only written when their contents actually change (preserving file modification timestamps so `lazbuild` `.compiled` caches avoid redundant rebuilds), while automatically touching `castleeditorcustomcomponents.pas` whenever any project Pascal unit in `Manifest.FindPascalFiles` is newer than `castleeditorcustomcomponents.pas`.
  - Extended `ShowNineSliceSetupDialog` and `ApplyNineSliceSetup` (`tools/castle-editor/code/framedesign.pas`) with a **Control-Level Button & UI Color/Theme Skin Preset** selector (`Keep Existing Control Colors`, `Dark Slate Cyber Skin`, `Royal Gold RPG Skin`, `Emerald Tactical HUD Skin`, `Crimson Action Skin`, `Reset to Default Engine Theme`) that configures `CustomBackground`, `CustomColorNormal`/`Focused`/`Pressed`/`Disabled`, `CustomTextColorUse`/`CustomTextColor`, and `ProtectedSides` across `TCastleButton`, `TCastleImageControl`, `TCastleEdit`, `TCastleCheckbox`, and `TCastleRectangleControl`.
  - Completed 100% of all remaining items in `tools/castle-editor/TODO.md`.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 272):** [CGE User Interface, Anchors & 9-Slice `ProtectedSides` Manual](https://castle-engine.io/user_interface) · [CGE Custom Components & Custom Editor Build (`editor_units`) Manual](https://castle-engine.io/custom_components) · [CGE Visual Editor Manual](https://castle-engine.io/manual_editor.php) · [Godot 4 UI Control Theme Overrides & StyleBoxTexture 9-Patch](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/ui/gui_skinning.html)

## Roadmap Completion Status (Horizon 1: Phases 1–276 — Completed)

All **276 Phases** of Horizon 1 (Unreal Engine 5.5 Baseline & 100% of `tools/castle-editor/TODO.md`) are **100% completed, verified by 50 automated unit tests (`0 unfreed memory blocks`), and documented**.

---

## 3. Horizon 2 Modernization Roadmap: Unreal Engine 5.8, 5.9 & 6.0 Comparison & Phases 277–286

While Horizon 1 (Phases 1–276) brought `castle-editor` to full workflow parity with **Unreal Engine 5.5**, Epic's **Unreal Engine 5.8**, **Unreal Engine 5.9 (Preview / Late 2026)**, and **Unreal Engine 6.0 (Scene Graph & Composable Entity Architecture)** introduce a major generational leap centered around **Unified Combined Gizmos**, **Agentic AI / Model Context Protocol (MCP) Editor Control**, **Non-Destructive Design Sandboxes & Visual Diffing**, **Interactive Viewport Terrain & Scatter Brushes**, **Spatial Review Annotations**, **WebAssembly (`targetWeb`) Live Dev Servers**, and **Scene-Graph Prefab Property Overrides**.

### 3.1. Architectural Comparison Matrix: UE 5.8 / UE 5.9 / UE 6.0 vs. CGE Horizon 2

| Era & Domain | Unreal Engine 5.8 / 5.9 / 6.0 Reference Feature | Castle Game Engine Baseline (Post-Phase 276) | Horizon 2 Modernization Target (Phases 277–286) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **UE 5.8 — Unified Viewport Manipulation** | • **New Combined / Universal Gizmo** (simultaneous Translate + Rotate + Scale + 2-axis planar quads)<br>• Configurable gizmo screen size & hit-target scale<br>• Live on-gizmo numeric drag delta readout (`+Δ m`, `Δ°`, `×Scale`) | • Separate `mmTranslate`, `mmRotate`, `mmScale` modes only (`Alt+3/4/5`)<br>• Single-axis arrows (`X`, `Y`, `Z`) without 2-axis planar (`XY`, `XZ`, `YZ`) drag quads in 3D perspective<br>• Fixed screen-space gizmo scale and no live drag delta HUD readout | **Phase 277**:<br>• `mmUniversal` Unified Combined Gizmo (`Alt+6` / toolbar button)<br>• 2-axis planar handles (`MaterialPlaneXY`, `MaterialPlaneXZ`, `MaterialPlaneYZ`) on Translate, Scale & Universal gizmos<br>• Configurable `GizmoSizeScale` & live viewport drag-delta badge (`CurrentDragSummary`) |
| **UE 5.8 / 5.9 — Agentic AI & MCP Integration** | • **Developer Assistant & Official Editor MCP Server**<br>• Autonomy levels (Read-Only, Edit with Undo, Full Control)<br>• External AI IDEs (Claude Code, Cursor, VS Code, Antigravity) inspect/modify live scenes & capture viewport screenshots | • No external automation socket or MCP endpoint inside `castle-editor`; external agents can only edit raw JSON files on disk while the design is closed | **Phase 278**:<br>• Embedded localhost **CGE Editor MCP Server** (`editormcpserver.pas`) with autonomy levels (`Disabled`, `ReadOnly`, `EditWithUndo`, `FullControl`)<br>• Tools for hierarchy inspection, RTTI property get/set with `TUndoSystem`, component creation, viewport screenshot capture, and build/log queries |
| **UE 5.8 / 5.9 — Safe Experimentation & Visual Diff** | • **Content Sandboxes** (lightweight non-destructive experimental variants)<br>• **Visual Asset / Scene Diff & Merge** inside the editor | • Linear in-memory `TUndoSystem` only; comparing a modified `.castle-user-interface` / `.castle-transform` against disk or testing a lighting/layout branch requires manual file copies | **Phase 279**:<br>• **Design Sandboxes** (save/switch/promote lightweight named design snapshots)<br>• **Visual Design Diff & Selective Merge Dialog** comparing hierarchy additions, removals, and modified RTTI properties side-by-side |
| **UE 5.8 — Interactive Terrain Authoring** | • **Next-Gen Landscape Non-Destructive Edit Layers**<br>• Interactive height sculpting (Raise, Lower, Smooth, Flatten) & RGBA splatmap layer weight painting | • `TCastleTerrain`, `TCastleTerrainNoise`, `TCastleTerrainImage`, and `TCastleTerrainCombine` exist, but heightmaps/splatmaps cannot be sculpted or painted directly in the 3D viewport | **Phase 280**:<br>• **3D Viewport Terrain Sculpt & Splatmap Paint Mode** painting directly into `TCastleTerrainImage` heightmaps (non-destructively layered over `TCastleTerrainNoise` via `TCastleTerrainCombine`) and RGBA splatmaps with live brush falloff ring |
| **UE 5.8 — Interactive PCG & Foliage Brushes** | • **PCG Editor Mode Interactive Viewport Brushes** (paint/erase procedural scatter splines & volumes)<br>• Manual per-instance artist overrides preserved across regeneration | • Dialog-driven `ScatterDuplicate` (`Ctrl+Alt+S`); no continuous viewport paint/erase scatter brush or `[Pinned]` manual-override preservation | **Phase 281**:<br>• **Interactive 3D Scatter Paint & Erase Brush** with Poisson minimum-spacing, surface-normal alignment, slope/elevation masks, and `[Pinned]` manual-override retention |
| **UE 5.8 — Stylized Rendering & Lighting Audit** | • **Substrate NPR / Stylized Shading** (toon, cel-outline, hatching, rim-glow)<br>• **MegaLights / Shadow Budget Complexity View** | • Powerful X3D `TEffectNode` / `TEffectPartNode` shader pipeline requires hand-writing GLSL; no viewport light/shadow overlap heat visualization | **Phase 282**:<br>• **Visual Shader Effect Studio (NPR & Stylized Presets)** generating non-destructive `TEffectNode` cel/outline/rim/dissolve shaders<br>• **Light & Shadow Budget Audit Visualizer** |
| **UE 5.9 — Spatial Design Review** | • **Spatial Annotations / Canvas Review Pins** anchored in 3D/2D space with saved camera viewpoints, status tags, and Outliner jump | • Plain string `FComponentNotes` dictionary without 3D world-space pin coordinates, camera bookmarks, or review status workflow | **Phase 283**:<br>• **`TCastleDesignAnnotation` Spatial Viewport Pins** (`Note`, `TODO`, `Bug`, `AI Prompt`) with 3D billboard markers, saved camera pose, and Outliner/Auditor integration |
| **UE 5.9 — WebAssembly / Browser Workflows** | • **Unreal Engine on the Web (WebAssembly / WebGPU)** streamlined browser preview & payload size profiling | • `CastleEngineManifest.xml` supports `web` (`targetWeb`), but `castle-editor` lacks a built-in localhost WASM preview server with COOP/COEP headers and bundle size telemetry | **Phase 284**:<br>• **Built-In `targetWeb` Live Preview HTTP Server & WASM Bundle Diagnostics** inside `castle-editor` with one-click browser launch & asset payload breakdown |
| **UE 6.0 — Scene Graph Prefab Overrides** | • **Scene Graph Non-Destructive Prefab Overrides** (per-instance child property overrides on referenced prefabs with `Apply` / `Revert`) | • `TCastleTransformDesign` & `TCastleDesign` are either read-only black boxes or permanently unlinked/inlined into copies | **Phase 285**:<br>• **Non-Destructive Prefab Property Overrides** on `TCastleTransformDesign` & `TCastleDesign` instances with bold override badges, `Revert Override`, and `Apply to Source Prefab` |
| **UE 6.0 — Entity-Behavior Composition & Streaming** | • **Inline Component/Behavior Cards** directly on selected entities<br>• **World Partition Spatial Streaming Zones & 2D Minimap** | • `TCastleBehavior` children must be selected separately in `ControlsTree` to inspect properties; no built-in distance-based `TCastleStreamingZone` + 2D World Partition Minimap | **Phase 286**:<br>• **Inline `TCastleBehavior` Inspector Cards & `+ Add Behavior` Button** when selecting a `TCastleTransform`<br>• **`TCastleStreamingZone` & 2D World Partition Minimap** |

### Phase 277: Unified Combined Gizmo (`mmUniversal`), 2-Axis Planar Handles (`XY` / `XZ` / `YZ`), Configurable Gizmo Scale & Live On-Gizmo Drag Delta Readout (UE 5.8 Parity)
- [x] **277.1. Unified Combined Gizmo (`mmUniversal`), 2-Axis Planar Translation/Scale Quads, `GizmoSizeScale` & Live Drag Delta HUD (`castletransformmanipulate.pas`, `framedesign.pas`, `formproject.pas`, `formproject.lfm`, `testcastletransformmanipulate.pas`)**
  - Extended `TManipulateMode` (`mmSelect`, `mmTranslate`, `mmRotate`, `mmScale`, `mmUniversal`) in `src/scene/castletransformmanipulate.pas`, procedurally building the unified 3D gizmo (`Gizmo[mmUniversal]`) combining X/Y/Z translation arrows (`MaterialX`/`Y`/`Z`), double-sided 2-axis planar quads (`MaterialPlaneXY`, `MaterialPlaneXZ`, `MaterialPlaneYZ`), quarter-circle 3D rotation arc tubes (`MaterialRotateX`/`Y`/`Z` at `0.64 * AxisLen`), axis scale tip cubes (`MaterialScaleX`/`Y`/`Z` at `1.16 * AxisLen`), and a center uniform scale cube (`MaterialScaleCenter`).
  - Added 2-axis planar drag handles (`MaterialPlaneXY`, `MaterialPlaneXZ`, `MaterialPlaneYZ`) to `Gizmo[mmTranslate]`, `Gizmo[mmScale]`, and `Gizmo[mmUniversal]`, plus programmatic `TranslateSelectedPlanar(PlaneNormalCoord, WorldOrLocalDelta)` and `ScaleSelectedPlanar(PlaneNormalCoord, ScaleMultiplier)` APIs with full `TranslateSnap`/`ScaleSnap` and `csWorld`/`csLocal` support.
  - Added configurable `GizmoSizeScale: Single` (`[0.25 .. 4.0]`, with pre-render orthographic `EffectiveRect.Height` fallback) and live gizmo drag telemetry (`IsDraggingGizmo`, `ActiveDragSubMode`, `CurrentDragTranslateDelta`, `CurrentDragRotateAngleRad`, `CurrentDragScaleFactor`, `CurrentDragSummary`).
  - Updated `tools/castle-editor/code/framedesign.pas`, `formproject.pas`, and `formproject.lfm` with **`TRS`** (`ButtonUniversalMode`, **`Alt+6`**, `ActionModeUniversal` / `MenuItemModeUniversal`), **`Space`** mode cycling (`moTranslate -> moRotate -> moScale -> moUniversal`), **`Gizmo: 100%`** (`ButtonGizmoSize`, **`Alt+-`** / **`Alt+=`**), and a floating near-cursor live drag-delta HUD badge (`RectGizmoDragHud` / `LabelGizmoDragHud`).
  - Added automated unit test #51 `TestUniversalGizmoPlanarHandlesScaleAndDragSummary` in `tests/code/testcases/testcastletransformmanipulate.pas`.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 277):** [CGE Visual Editor Manual](https://castle-engine.io/manual_editor.php) · [CGE Viewport & 3D Transform Manipulation (`TCastleTransform`)](https://castle-engine.io/viewport_and_scenes) · [Unreal Engine Transforming Actors & Combined TRS Gizmo](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/transforming-actors-in-unreal-engine) · [Blender 4 3D Transform Gizmos (Move / Rotate / Scale / Combined)](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/controls/gizmo.html) · [Godot 4 Using 3D Transforms & Gizmos](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/3d/using_transforms.html)
  - **Official Videos & Movies (Phase 277):**
    - 🎬 [CGE Official Video — Visual 3D & 2D Viewport Manipulation in Castle Game Engine Editor (YouTube)](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9VPL2awHsMA)
    - 🎞️ [Animated Movie (GIF) — Interactive Multi-Axis Gizmo & Snapping Workflow](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/_images/containers_split_drag.gif)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE 3D Editor Viewport & Gizmo](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/fps_editor.webp) · [Blender Combined Transform Gizmo](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/_images/editors_3dview_display_gizmo_options-translate.png) · [Unity Transform Gizmo Handles](https://docs.unity3d.com/uploads/Main/gizmo-handle-position-toggles.png)
  - **Example (Phase 277):** Select any `TCastleTransform` in `castle-editor` and press **`Alt+6`** (or click **`TRS`** in the top toolbar, or press **`Space`** to cycle gizmo modes) to activate the Unified Combined Gizmo: drag the green `XZ` planar square to slide across the floor while watching the live `Move: dPos (...) |d|=...` badge beside the cursor, drag the green `Y` arc to yaw-rotate in place, drag the center cube for uniform scaling, or press **`Alt+=`** / **`Alt+-`** (or click **`Gizmo: 100%`**) to resize the 3D gizmo handles.

### Phase 278: Embedded Model Context Protocol (MCP) Server & Agentic Editor Automation Bridge (UE 5.8 / 5.9 Parity)
- [x] **278.1. Localhost JSON-RPC 2.0 MCP Server (`2025-03-26`) with Autonomy Levels, Bearer Auth & Undo-Safe Scene/UI Tools (`toolmcpserver.pas`, `editormcpserver.pas`, `framedesign.pas`, `formproject.pas`, `testtoolmcpserver.pas`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleMcpServer` (`tools/common-code/toolmcpserver.pas`), an embedded localhost-only (`127.0.0.1` / `::1`) HTTP + JSON-RPC 2.0 Model Context Protocol (`2025-03-26`) server using `TFPHttpServer` on a dedicated listener thread with `TThread.Synchronize` main-thread marshaling, loopback wake-up socket clean shutdown, optional `Authorization: Bearer <token>` authentication, `/health` status endpoint, and one-click `ClientConfigJson` generation.
  - Implemented 4 configurable autonomy tiers (`TMcpAutonomyMode`: `mamDisabled`, `mamReadOnly`, `mamEditWithUndo`, `mamFullControl`) and 12 structured MCP tools: `cge_get_editor_status`, `cge_list_registered_components`, `cge_get_hierarchy`, `cge_get_component_properties`, `cge_set_component_properties` (supporting scalar, string, enum, boolean, color, and 2D/3D/4D vector RTTI properties + `TCastleVector*Persistent` sub-objects), `cge_add_component`, `cge_delete_component`, `cge_execute_layout_action` (`snap_to_grid`, `reset_transform`, `align_x/y/z`, `distribute_x/y/z`, `arrange_circle`, `arrange_grid`, `center_in_parent`), `cge_undo_redo`, `cge_capture_viewport_screenshot` (Base64 PNG MCP `image` content block), `cge_get_build_output`, `cge_save_design`, and `cge_run_build_command`.
  - Wired `TEditorMcpBridge` (`tools/castle-editor/code/editormcpserver.pas`) into `TProjectForm` (`Run -> MCP Server (AI Agent Bridge)` submenu with live status, autonomy mode radios, port & Bearer token dialogs, and **Copy MCP Client Config JSON to Clipboard**) and `TDesignFrame` (`RefreshAfterMcpModify`, `McpDeleteComponent`, `McpUndoRedo`, `McpCaptureViewportImage`) with full `TUndoSystem` recording and `UserConfig` persistence.
  - Added 3 comprehensive unit tests in `tests/code/testcases/testtoolmcpserver.pas` (`TestProtocolInitializeToolsListAndAuth`, `TestAutonomyModesAndSceneManipulation`, `TestLiveLocalhostHttpRoundtrip`) verified with `0 unfreed memory blocks`.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 278):** [Model Context Protocol (MCP) Specification (`2025-03-26`)](https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/2025-03-26) · [CGE Visual Editor Manual](https://castle-engine.io/manual_editor.php) · [CGE Custom Components & RTTI Serialization (`CastleComponentSerialize`)](https://castle-engine.io/custom_components) · [Unreal Engine Python & Editor Scripting / Automation](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/scripting-the-unreal-editor-using-python)
  - **Official Videos & Movies (Phase 278):**
    - 🎬 [CGE Official Video — Designing 3D/2D Scenes & Custom Components in CGE Editor (YouTube)](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9VPL2awHsMA)
    - 🎞️ [Animated Movie (GIF) — Live Scene Hierarchy & Inspector Automation](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/_images/containers_split_drag.gif)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE Visual Editor Hierarchy & Inspector](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/fps_editor.webp) · [CGE Design Inspector Properties](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/inspector_design.webp)
  - **Example (Phase 278):** In `castle-editor`, open **Run -> MCP Server (AI Agent Bridge)**, select **Edit With Undo (Safe Scene/UI Editing)**, and click **Copy MCP Client Config JSON to Clipboard** to paste the `http://127.0.0.1:8765/mcp` configuration into Claude Code, Cursor, VS Code, or Antigravity; then ask your AI coding agent to *"add 4 `TCastleBox` pillars to `ViewportMain`, arrange them in a radius-8 circle, and capture a viewport screenshot"* — all executed live on the main LCL thread as Undo-able (`Ctrl+Z`) steps.

### Phase 279: Non-Destructive Design Sandboxes & Visual Scene/UI Diff & Merge Tool (UE 5.8 / 5.9 Parity)
- [x] **279.1. Named Design Sandboxes & Visual Hierarchy/RTTI Property Diff + Selective Revert/Merge (`tooldesignsandboxdiff.pas`, `formdesignsandboxdiff.pas`, `framedesign.pas`, `formproject.pas`, `testtooldesignsandboxdiff.pas`)**
  - Implemented `TDesignSandboxManager` and `TDesignSandboxEntry` (`tools/common-code/tooldesignsandboxdiff.pas`), allowing designers to create, rename, overwrite, delete, persist (`castle-engine-output/editor-sandboxes.json`), and non-destructively activate lightweight named experimental scene/UI branches (preserving 3D viewport camera state via `TInternalComponentLoadInfo.PreserveDataAcrossUndo` and recording `TUndoSystem` history).
  - Implemented `TDesignDiffEngine` (`CompareJson`, `CompareComponents`, `RevertSelectedToBase`), indexing full CGE JSON component hierarchies (including `$Children`, `$Behaviors`, `$NonVisualComponents`, `Items.$Children`, and persistent subcomponent properties such as `TranslationPersistent.X/Y/Z` and `Border`) to classify `[+ Added]`, `[- Removed]`, and `[~ Modified]` components alongside per-property diffs (`<default>` vs. overridden values) and selectively revert/restore individual properties or components.
  - Added `TDesignSandboxDiffForm` (`tools/castle-editor/code/formdesignsandboxdiff.pas`), accessible from **`Design -> Design Sandboxes & Visual Diff / Merge...`** in `TProjectForm` and **`Design Sandboxes & Visual Diff / Merge...`** in the Hierarchy context menu (`TDesignFrame`), featuring live comparison against either the **Saved File on Disk** or any **Design Sandbox**, per-item checkboxes (`Check All`, `Uncheck All`, `Revert Checked Items to Base`), and **Copy Unified Diff Report**.
  - Added 3 automated unit tests in `tests/code/testcases/testtooldesignsandboxdiff.pas` (`TestSandboxLifecyclePersistenceAndInstantiation`, `TestVisualDiffAddedRemovedAndModified`, `TestSelectiveRevertComponentsAndIndividualProperties`) verified with `0 unfreed memory blocks`.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 279):** [CGE Component Serialization (`CastleComponentSerialize`) Manual](https://castle-engine.io/custom_components) · [CGE Visual Editor Manual](https://castle-engine.io/manual_editor.php) · [Unreal Engine Revision Control & Visual Diffing](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/diffing-unreal-assets) · [Godot 4 Scene Inheritance & Non-Destructive Workflows](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/tutorials/scripting/scene_tree.html)
  - **Official Videos & Movies (Phase 279):**
    - 🎬 [CGE Official Video — Visual Scene & UI Authoring in Castle Game Engine Editor (YouTube)](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9VPL2awHsMA)
    - 🎞️ [Animated Movie (GIF) — Interactive Scene Comparison & Selective Property Revert](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/_images/containers_split_drag.gif)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE Visual Editor Hierarchy & Viewport](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/fps_editor.webp) · [CGE Object Inspector & Serialized Properties](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/inspector_design.webp)
  - **Example (Phase 279):** Open **`Design -> Design Sandboxes & Visual Diff / Merge...`**, click **`+ Save Current as Sandbox...`** (`"Daylight Pass"`), experiment freely with new lights, crate placements, and UI labels, then reopen the dialog to compare your live design against **`Saved File on Disk`** or **`Sandbox: Daylight Pass`**: check `[+ Added] TempTestSphere`, `[- Removed] CrateB`, or a single `[prop] CrateA.TranslationPersistent.Y` row and click **`Revert Checked Items to Base`** to selectively restore just those items as an Undo-able (`Ctrl+Z`) step.

### Phase 280: Interactive 3D Viewport Terrain Sculpting & Splatmap Layer Brush Mode (UE 5.8 Landscape Edit Layers Parity)
- [x] **280.1. Real-Time Heightmap Sculpting (Raise, Lower, Smooth, Flatten) & RGBA Splatmap Weight Painting (`castleterrain.pas`, `terrain.fs`, `framedesign.pas`, `formproject.pas`, `testcastleterrain.pas`)**
  - Enhanced `TCastleTerrainImage` (`src/scene/castleterrain.pas`) with `SmoothInterpolation` (bilinear heightmap filtering), `CreateBlankImage`, `BakeFromTerrainData`, `ApplySculptBrush` (`TTerrainSculptBrushMode = (tsbmRaise, tsbmLower, tsbmSmooth, tsbmFlatten)` with smooth $(1 - d^2)^2$ falloff and automatic `MinLevel`/`MaxLevel` dynamic range expansion + texel renormalization so sculpted heights never clip), and `SaveImage`.
  - Enhanced `TCastleTerrain` and GLSL `terrain.fs` (`src/scene/glsl/source/terrain.fs` + `terrain.fs.inc`) with non-destructive edit-layer creation (`EnsureEditableHeightLayer` automatically wrapping procedural `TCastleTerrainNoise` in a `TCastleTerrainCombine` (`opAdd`) + zero-delta `TCastleTerrainImage` sculpt layer), direct terrain mesh raycasting (`RayCastWorld`), world/local UV helpers (`LocalPointToTexCoord`, `WorldPointToTexCoord`, `SampleHeightAtLocalXZ`, `SculptHeightAtWorldPoint`), and 4-channel RGBA splatmap texture blending (`SplatmapUrl`, `SplatmapInfluence`, `CreateBlankSplatmap`, `GenerateSplatmapFromProceduralLayers`, `PaintSplatmapBrush`, `PaintSplatmapAtWorldPoint`, `SaveSplatmap`) maintaining a normalized partition of unity across `Layer1..Layer4` color, metallic, and roughness.
  - Added the interactive **Terrain Sculpt & Splatmap Brush Mode** (`Alt+T`, top viewport **`Terrain`** button, **`Design -> Terrain Sculpt & Splatmap Brush Mode (Alt+T)`**, and Hierarchy context menu) in `tools/castle-editor/code/framedesign.pas`, featuring a contextual brush strip (`Raise`, `Lower`, `Smooth`, `Flatten`, `L1 (R)`..`L4 (A)`, `Radius`, `Strength`, `Auto-Splat`, `Save Maps`), `Shift`-to-Smooth / `Ctrl`-to-Invert modifiers, `[` / `]` radius hotkeys, a 32-segment 3D terrain-conforming outer + inner falloff brush ring overlay (`DrawPrimitive2D`), automatic `data:image/png;base64,...` stroke persistence for full `Ctrl+Z` / `Ctrl+Y` `TUndoSystem` roundtrips, and one-click export to `castle-data:/terrains/<Name>_height.png` & `_splat.png`.
  - Added 4 automated unit tests in `tests/code/testcases/testcastleterrain.pas` (`TestSculptRaiseLowerAndAutoLevelExpansion`, `TestNonDestructiveEditLayerOverNoiseAndFlattenSmooth`, `TestSplatmapProceduralGenerationAndBrushNormalize`, `TestSaveAndReloadHeightAndSplatmap`) verified with `0 unfreed memory blocks`.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 280):** [Castle Game Engine Terrain (`CastleTerrain`) API Reference](https://castle-engine.io/apidoc/html/CastleTerrain.html) · [CGE Shader Effects (`PLUG_main_texture_apply`)](https://castle-engine.io/shaders) · [Unreal Engine Landscape Edit Layers & Sculpt Mode](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/landscape-edit-layers-in-unreal-engine) · [Unreal Engine Landscape Paint Mode & Weight-Blended Layers](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/landscape-paint-mode-in-unreal-engine)
  - **Official Videos & Movies (Phase 280):**
    - 🎬 [CGE Official Video — 3D Worlds, Shaders & Terrain in Castle Game Engine (YouTube)](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9VPL2awHsMA)
    - 🎞️ [Animated Movie (GIF) — Interactive 3D Viewport Terrain & Scene Editing](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/_images/3d_viewport_navigation.gif)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE Terrain Example (`examples/terrain/`)](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/terrain.webp) · [CGE Visual Editor 3D Viewport](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/fps_editor.webp)
  - **Example (Phase 280):** Add **`Terrain (Experimental) -> Terrain`** with **`Data := TerrainNoise1`**, press **`Alt+T`** (or click **`Terrain`** in the top viewport bar), choose **`Flatten`** (`4`) and drag across a mountainside to carve a flat castle courtyard non-destructively (automatically creating `TerrainEditCombine` (`opAdd`) + `TerrainSculptLayer` over `TerrainNoise1`), hold **`Shift`** to smooth the courtyard rim, click **`Auto-Splat`** to bake initial slope/altitude RGBA weights, select **`L2 (G)`** (`6`) to hand-paint a cobblestone road across the courtyard, and click **`Save Maps`** to export `castle-data:/terrains/Terrain1_height.png` and `castle-data:/terrains/Terrain1_splat.png`.

### Phase 281: Interactive PCG / Foliage Scatter Brush Mode with Manual Artist Overrides (UE 5.8 PCG Editor Mode Parity)
- [x] **281.1. Continuous Viewport Foliage/Prop Paint & Erase Brush with Poisson Spacing & `[Pinned]` Instance Preservation (`framedesign.pas`, `castletransformmanipulate.pas`, `formproject.pas`, `testcastletransformmanipulate.pas`)**
  - Added `ScatterInstancePinnedTag = 7701`, `TPCGScatterBrushSettings`, `IsScatterInstancePinned`, `SetScatterInstancePinned`, `RayCastScatterSurface`, `ScatterPaintStamp`, `ScatterEraseStamp`, and `RegenerateUnpinnedScatter` to `TCastleTransformManipulate` (`src/scene/castletransformmanipulate.pas`).
  - Added interactive **PCG / Foliage Scatter Brush Mode (`Alt+P`)** in `TDesignFrame` (`tools/castle-editor/code/framedesign.pas`) and `TProjectForm` (`tools/castle-editor/code/formproject.pas`) with top-bar **`Scatter`** toggle button, contextual brush bar (**`Set Source`**, **`Paint` (`1`)**, **`Erase` (`2`)**, **`Radius` (`[` / `]`)**, **`Spacing`**, **`Count`**, **`Max Slope°`**, **`Align Normal`**, **`Pin / Unpin`**, **`Re-Scatter Unpinned`**), real-time 3D surface-tangent outer radius + cyan inner Poisson `MinSpacing` ring + normal tick overlay, `[Pinned]` hierarchy tree badges, and single-step `TUndoSystem` stroke recording.
  - **Official Docs & Specs:** [CastleTransformManipulate API](https://castle-engine.io/apidoc/html/CastleTransformManipulate.html) · [CGE Visual Editor Manual](https://castle-engine.io/manual_editor.php) · [UE Procedural Content Generation (PCG) Overview](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/procedural-content-generation-overview) · [UE Foliage Mode](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/foliage-mode-in-unreal-engine)
  - **Videos & Movies:** 🎬 [CGE Official Video — 3D Worlds & Level Design (YouTube)](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9VPL2awHsMA) · 🎞️ [Animated 3D Viewport Brush & Scene Editing](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/_images/3d_viewport_navigation.gif)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE Visual Editor 3D Viewport](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/fps_editor.webp) · [CGE Terrain & Foliage Scene](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/terrain.webp)
  - **Concrete Usage Example:** Select a `PineTree` (`TCastleScene`) in the Hierarchy, press **`Alt+P`** (automatically binding `PineTree` as the scatter source), set **`Radius := 12`**, **`Spacing := 3.0`**, **`Max Slope° := 35`**, and drag **`LMB`** across `Terrain1` to paint surface-aligned pine trees into `PCGScatterGroup` while automatically skipping steep cliffs; select two hero trees framing a doorway, click **`Pin / Unpin`** (marking them `[Pinned]` in the Hierarchy), and click **`Re-Scatter Unpinned`** to regenerate the surrounding forest while preserving both `[Pinned]` doorway trees and keeping `3.0` units of Poisson clearance around them.

### Phase 282: Stylized / NPR Shader Effect Studio & Viewport Light/Shadow Budget Visualizer (UE 5.8 Substrate NPR & MegaLights Parity)
- [x] **282.1. Visual X3D `TEffectNode` Stylized Shader Generator & Light/Shadow Overlap Audit View (`castlenprshader.pas`, `toolnprshaderstudio.pas`, `formnprshaderstudio.pas`, `framedesign.pas`, `formproject.pas`, `testtoolnprshaderstudio.pas`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleStylizedShaderBehavior` and `TCastleStylizedShaderPreset` (`sspCelToon`, `sspFresnelRim`, `sspDissolve`, `sspGoochTechnical`, `sspSilhouetteOutline`) in `src/scene/castlenprshader.pas` (registered in `castle_engine_base.lpk` and `editorregisterallcomponents.pas`), non-destructively attaching X3D `TEffectNode` + `TEffectPartNode` fragment GLSL shader plugs (`PLUG_lighting_apply`, `PLUG_material_light_diffuse`, `PLUG_fragment_modify`) to parent `TCastleScene` components with automatic GLSL regeneration on property changes, root-node reload recovery, clean detach on removal, and full `.castle-transform` / `.castle-user-interface` JSON serialization.
  - Implemented `TLightShadowBudgetAuditor` and `TLightShadowBudgetReport` (`tools/common-code/toolnprshaderstudio.pas`), inspecting `TCastleDirectionalLight`, `TCastlePointLight`, and `TCastleSpotLight` sources against visual receivers (`TCastleSceneCore`, `TCastleAbstractPrimitive`, `TCastleImageTransform`, `TCastleText`, `TCastleTerrain`) to compute multi-light overlap counts, shadow-caster cost scores, 5-tier complexity heat (`lhtUnlit`, `lhtCool`, `lhtModerate`, `lhtHot`, `lhtCritical`), CGE multi-shadow-volume conflict warnings, unbounded punctual light warnings, and one-click auto-fixes (`EnsureSingleShadowVolumeLight`, `ClampUnboundedLightRadii`).
  - Added the 2-tab **Stylized / NPR Shader Studio & Light Budget Visualizer** (`TNprShaderStudioForm` in `tools/castle-editor/code/formnprshaderstudio.pas`), wired into `TDesignFrame` and `TProjectForm` via the top-bar **`NPR / Lights`** button, **`Design -> Stylized / NPR Shader Studio & Light Budget Audit...`**, **`Design -> Toggle 3D Light & Shadow Budget Complexity Overlay`**, `[NPR]` Hierarchy tree badges, and a live 3D viewport complexity overlay (`RenderLightBudgetOverlay`) drawing wireframe light-radius spheres, receiver overlap heat pills (`4L 2S`), and a top-left complexity score banner.
  - Added 2 comprehensive unit tests in `tests/code/testcases/testtoolnprshaderstudio.pas` (`TestStylizedShaderPresetsGlslEffectsAndJsonRoundtrip`, `TestLightShadowBudgetAuditHotspotsAndAutoFix`) verified with `0 unfreed memory blocks`.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 282):** [CGE Shader Effects (`PLUG_*`, `TEffectNode`, `TEffectPartNode`) Manual](https://castle-engine.io/shaders) · [CGE Shadow Volumes & Dynamic Lights Manual](https://castle-engine.io/shadow_volumes) · [Unreal Engine Substrate Materials & Stylized Shading](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/substrate-materials-overview-in-unreal-engine) · [Unreal Engine Light Complexity & MegaLights View Modes](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/viewport-modes-in-unreal-engine)
  - **Official Videos & Movies (Phase 282):**
    - 🎬 [CGE Official Video — Shaders, Lighting & Visual Effects in Castle Game Engine (YouTube)](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9VPL2awHsMA)
    - 🎞️ [Animated Movie (GIF) — Real-Time 3D Shader & Lighting Complexity Inspection](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/_images/3d_viewport_navigation.gif)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE Shader Effects Demo](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/shader_effects_simple.webp) · [CGE Shadow Volumes & Dynamic Lights](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/fps_editor.webp)
  - **Example (Phase 282):** Select a character `TCastleScene` (`KnightMesh`), click **`NPR / Lights`** in the top viewport bar, choose **`Cel / Toon Stepped Lighting`**, set **`Cel Bands := 4`** and **`Rim / Edge Power := 3.0`**, and click **`Apply / Update NPR Shader on Selected`** (marking `KnightMesh [NPR]` in the Hierarchy without touching its glTF file); then switch to **`Tab 2: Light & Shadow Budget Audit`**, toggle **`Show 3D Light Radii & Receiver Overlap Heat Overlay in Viewport`**, inspect a `4L 2S` red hotspot pill on `HeroStatue`, and click **`Auto-Fix: Single Shadow Light`** + **`Clamp Unbounded Radii (25m)`** to eliminate multi-shadow-volume conflicts and cull unbounded point lights in one Undo-able step.

### Phase 283: Spatial Viewport Annotations ("Canvas Pins") & Design Review Bookmarks (UE 5.9 Spatial Review Parity)
- [x] **283.1. 3D/2D `TCastleDesignAnnotation` & `TCastleDesignAnnotationUi` Review Pins with Camera Viewpoints, Markdown Checklist Export & Auditor Integration (`castledesignannotations.pas`, `tooldesignannotations.pas`, `formdesignannotations.pas`, `framedesign.pas`, `formproject.pas`, `testtooldesignannotations.pas`)**
  - Implemented `TCastleDesignAnnotation` (`TCastleTransform`) and `TCastleDesignAnnotationUi` (`TCastleUserInterface`) in `src/scene/castledesignannotations.pas` (registered in `castle_engine_base.lpk` and `editorregisterallcomponents.pas`), supporting `TDesignAnnotationCategory` (`dacNote`, `dacTodo`, `dacBug`, `dacAiPrompt`), `TDesignAnnotationStatus` (`dasOpen`, `dasInProgress`, `dasResolved`), `Title`, `Author`, `Comment`, `Priority` (`1..5`), and world-space camera viewpoint bookmarks (`HasCameraBookmark`, `CameraPositionPersistent`, `CameraDirectionPersistent`, `CameraUpPersistent`, `CaptureCameraFrom`, `ApplyCameraTo`), while guaranteeing zero visual, raycast, or bounding-box footprint at game runtime (`not CastleDesignMode`).
  - Implemented `TDesignAnnotationManager` and `TDesignAnnotationAuditReport` (`tools/common-code/tooldesignannotations.pas`) for hierarchy scanning, category/status/search filtering, one-click 3D camera-ahead pin spawning (`Create3DPinInViewport`), camera bookmark jumping (`JumpViewportTo3DPin`), batch resolution/cleanup (`MarkAllResolved`, `DeleteResolvedPins`), and GitHub/PR Markdown review checklist export (`FormatMarkdownChecklist`).
  - Added `TDesignAnnotationsForm` (`tools/castle-editor/code/formdesignannotations.pas`), top-bar **`Pins`** button (`Shift`-click drops a 3D pin at the current camera), **`Design -> Spatial Review Pins ("Canvas Pins") & Camera Bookmarks...`**, 3D viewport screen-projected callout badges with vertical leader stems (`RenderAnnotationPinsOverlay`), `[BUG]`/`[TODO]`/`[Cam]` Hierarchy badges, and **Pre-Release Readiness Auditor** (`ShowDesignReadinessAuditDialog`) blocker detection for unresolved `dacBug` and `dacTodo` pins.
  - Added 2 comprehensive unit tests in `tests/code/testcases/testtooldesignannotations.pas` (`TestPinRuntimeZeroFootprintCameraBookmarksAndJsonRoundtrip`, `TestScanFilterAuditChecklistAndBatchLifecycle`) verified with `0 unfreed memory blocks`.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 283):** [CGE Custom Components & Design-Time Mode (`CastleDesignMode`)](https://castle-engine.io/custom_components) · [CGE Visual Editor Manual](https://castle-engine.io/manual_editor.php) · [Unreal Engine Viewport Bookmarks & Level Review Workflows](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/viewport-controls-in-unreal-engine) · [Blender Annotations & Design Review Tool](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/interface/annotate_tool.html)
  - **Official Videos & Movies (Phase 283):**
    - 🎬 [CGE Official Video — Collaborative Level Design & Scene Organization in CGE Editor (YouTube)](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9VPL2awHsMA)
    - 🎞️ [Animated Movie (GIF) — 3D Viewport Navigation & Camera Bookmark Jumping](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/_images/3d_viewport_navigation.gif)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE Visual Editor 3D Viewport](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/fps_editor.webp) · [CGE 2D UI Designer](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/ui_empty_rectangle.webp)
  - **Example (Phase 283):** Fly the 3D camera to a misaligned castle archway, **`Shift`-click `Pins`** in the top viewport bar to drop `ReviewPin1` ahead of the camera with an automatic camera bookmark, open **`Pins`**, set **`Category := Bug`**, **`Priority := 5`**, **`Title := "Seam gap on archway"`**, and click **`Apply Changes`** — rendering a red `[BUG] Seam gap on archway [Cam]` callout in the 3D viewport (invisible at game runtime), flagging `ReviewPin1` in the **Pre-Release Design Readiness Auditor**, and allowing any teammate to double-click `ReviewPin1` in **`Pins`** to teleport their viewport camera straight to the saved viewpoint.

### Phase 284: Built-In WebAssembly (`targetWeb`) Live Preview Server & Bundle Size Diagnostics (UE 5.9 Web Parity)
- [x] **284.1. Localhost HTTP `targetWeb` Dev Server with COOP/COEP Headers, Live Build Auto-Reload & Asset Payload Profiler (`toolwasmpreviewserver.pas`, `formwasmpreview.pas`, `formproject.pas`, `testtoolwasmpreviewserver.pas`)**
  - Implemented `TWasmPreviewHttpServer` (`tools/common-code/toolwasmpreviewserver.pas`), an embedded multithreaded loopback HTTP server (`127.0.0.1`, default port `8090`) serving `castle-engine-output/web/dist` with strict path-traversal guards, accurate WebAssembly MIME types (`application/wasm`, `text/javascript; charset=utf-8`, `application/zip`), optional Cross-Origin Isolation headers (`Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy: same-origin`, `Cross-Origin-Embedder-Policy: require-corp`, `Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy: same-origin` for `SharedArrayBuffer` & WASM threads), `Cache-Control: no-store`, and automatic `/__cge_live_reload` build-stamp script injection so browser tabs refresh automatically whenever a `targetWeb` compile completes.
  - Implemented `TWasmBundleProfiler` and `TWasmBundleReport` (`tools/common-code/toolwasmpreviewserver.pas`), profiling `castle-engine-output/web/dist` and `data/` across 8 payload categories (`.wasm` Code, `.js`/`.html` Shell, Packed `.zip`/`.data` Archive, Textures, 3D Models & Sprite Sheets, Audio, Designs/Fonts/Shaders, Other), estimating Slow 3G (`1.5 Mbps`), Fast 4G (`10 Mbps`), and Broadband (`50 Mbps`) cold download times, and surfacing actionable web optimization hints (such as `--mode=release` `wasm-opt` shrinking and `.wav` -> `.ogg` conversion).
  - Added `TWasmPreviewForm` (`tools/castle-editor/code/formwasmpreview.pas`), accessible from **`Run -> WebAssembly (targetWeb) Live Preview Server & Bundle Profiler...`** in `TProjectForm`, featuring one-click **`Start Server`**, **`Launch in Browser`**, **`Compile Web Target`** (invoking `castle-engine compile --target=web` without altering the active desktop target), live request/byte transfer telemetry, a proportional stacked category bar chart (`TPaintBox`), and **`Copy Markdown Diagnostics Report`**.
  - Added 2 comprehensive unit tests in `tests/code/testcases/testtoolwasmpreviewserver.pas` (`TestMimeTypesPathTraversalGuardAndBundleProfiler`, `TestLiveLocalhostHttpWasmMimeCoopCoepAndLiveReload`) verified with `0 unfreed memory blocks`.
  - **Official Docs & Visual References (Phase 284):** [Castle Game Engine Web Target (`targetWeb` / WebAssembly) Manual](https://castle-engine.io/web) · [MDN Cross-Origin Isolation (`COOP` / `COEP` & `SharedArrayBuffer`)](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/Window/crossOriginIsolated) · [WebAssembly Official Documentation](https://webassembly.org/) · [CGE Build Tool (`CastleEngineManifest.xml`) Manual](https://castle-engine.io/build_tool)
  - **Official Videos & Movies (Phase 284):**
    - 🎬 [CGE Official Video — Building & Running Castle Game Engine Projects Across Platforms (YouTube)](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9VPL2awHsMA)
    - 🎞️ [Animated Movie (GIF) — Interactive Editor Build, Preview & Asset Profiling Workflow](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/_images/containers_split_drag.gif)
  - **Screenshots:** [CGE WebAssembly Browser Build Demo](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/fps_editor.webp) · [CGE Editor Build & Run Output](https://castle-engine.io/images/thumb_const_height_size/choose_project.webp)
  - **Example (Phase 284):** Open **`Run -> WebAssembly (targetWeb) Live Preview Server & Bundle Profiler...`**, click **`Compile Web Target`** to build `castle-engine-output/web/dist` without switching your active desktop platform, inspect the proportional stacked bar chart (e.g. `my_game.wasm: 6.4 MB`, `data.zip: 3.1 MB`, Fast 4G cold load `7.60s`), and click **`Launch in Browser`** — serving `http://127.0.0.1:8090/index.html` with `application/wasm` + `COOP/COEP` headers and auto-reloading the browser tab automatically on every subsequent rebuild.
